## 1 Nov 2026 - E285 round 93: every Telegram message gets one readable look - header, kind and time line, bullets, quoted news, effects (notification-scheduler, real-trade, position-stocks)

**Your request (1 Nov):** restyle the Telegram messages - news first (market hours and after hours), then every trade message - so they look good, read easily, with whatever animation is possible. Text only: no trade, stop, size or entry rule is touched.
 * **New `tg_style.py` (pure, never raises; identical copy in `notification-scheduler-service/notification`, `real-trade-service`, `position-stocks-service`; added to IDENTICAL in `check_shared_copies.py`).** `render(title, text)` gives: a header (kind icon + the message's own first bold line, else the title), a small `KIND · Sun 01 Nov · 18:45 IST` line, a divider, then the body re-laid:
   - `Key: value` lines become `▸ Key · value`; P&L / net / profit / loss lines with a signed amount get a green or red dot.
   - `Entry ₹x | Target ₹y | Stop ₹z` lines (3+ fields, how the BUY-placed and exit messages are written) become one bullet per field.
   - News: each headline (the indented `_italic_` line under a symbol) becomes a quote block, with a blank line between items, for both the market-hours messages (`intraday_news.py`) and the after-hours / pre-market scans (`afterhours_scan.py`). No news code changed; the look comes from the shared layer.
   - Kinds (icon and label): NEWS, ALERT, TARGET HIT, STOP HIT, BUY, SELL / EXIT, REPORT, UPDATE - picked from the title and the first lines only.
   - **Bug fixed on the way:** text was sent as HTML without escaping, so a stray `<` or `&` (Dhan error text, a headline) made Telegram refuse the message and fall back to unformatted text, and `_italic_` markers showed as literal underscores. Text is now escaped (the `<b>` / `<i>` / `<code>` tags callers already write are kept) and `*bold*` / `_italic_` become tags.
 * **"Animation":** Telegram text cannot animate. What it offers is a message effect, shown in a private chat only: confetti on a TARGET HIT, a thumbs-up on a BUY (`message_effect_id`; never in a group, never on a loss or alert). If Telegram refuses the effect the send is retried once without it. Optional: set `TELEGRAM_DASHBOARD_URL` (https) to add an "Open Stockky" button under each message.
 * **Where it applies:** the central sender `notification/main.py::_send_telegram` (all services reach Telegram through it, with effect and button) and the two direct fallbacks in the services' `notifier.py` (same look, no effect). Long messages are still split on line boundaries before styling; a styled part over 4000 characters falls back to the old layout. Discord, Slack and CallMeBot text are unchanged.
 * **Switches (Calibration tab, notification service):** `TELEGRAM_STYLE_ENABLED` (off = the old plain layout) and `TELEGRAM_EFFECTS_ENABLED`, both live; `TELEGRAM_DASHBOARD_URL` is an environment setting. `ENV_REFERENCE.txt` regenerated, five files added to `FILE_MANIFEST.txt`, the two notifier entries accepted in the shared-copies baseline.
 * **Tests:** new `real-trade-service/tests/test_e285_tg_style.py` (9: kinds, header, bullets, key / value and P&L dots, news quotes, escaping, the long-render fallback, effects and switches, three copies identical) and `notification-scheduler-service/tests/test_e285_tg_style_send.py` (6: styled send and effect, group / off, refused-effect retry, https-only button, switch off, long message under 4096). The two old tests that pin the plain layout (`test_notifier.py`, `test_telegram_long_message_split.py`) now switch styling off in an autouse fixture.
 * **Ran here:** the 9 style tests; `_send_telegram` driven end to end with stubbed `httpx` / `fastapi` (styled, effect retry, switch off, 150-line message in 7 parts, longest 3915 chars); `scripts/test_control_kit.py` (20); `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies`, `gen_env_reference --check`, `check_merge_conflicts`; `py_compile`. **Not run (no pytest, httpx or fastapi in this sandbox):** the 6 send tests, the changed notifier tests, and any send to the real Telegram - the effect ids are Telegram's published ones but unproven against your bot, so check one TARGET HIT and one BUY on the VM.
**Deploy:** `docker compose up -d --build notification-scheduler-service real-trade-service position-stocks-service`. **VM check:** `bash scripts/docker_test.sh notification-scheduler-service` (and real-trade-service, position-stocks-service), then `curl -X POST <notification-service>/test` or wait for the next news scan and look at the message.
**Still open:** removal of the shadow history views; the half-sell probe, `NO_SETUP_MODE=live` readiness, Dhan's IP rules, the admin password change.

## 1 Nov 2026 - E285 round 92: Real Trade risk ladder stage 3 - a setup whose stage 2 has lasted 60 trading days gets the 8 % heat limit (real-trade)

**Open item taken:** "the Real Trade risk ladder beyond stage 2" (round 59 built stages 0 to 2 only). The stage rules were already in the shared `entry_engine/risk_ladder.py` (identical copy, unchanged); Real Trade just never read the older trades.
 * `entry_engine/risk_stage.py`: new `older_cells(rows)` = the setup cells computed only from counted trades opened BEFORE the last 60 trading days (`risk_ladder.SUSTAIN_DAYS`; the 60th most recent trading day is the cutoff). It needs more than 60 distinct trading days, else `{}` (no stage 3). `_load` stores it as `older`; `stage_for` no longer caps at 2 and passes the setup's older cell to `risk_ladder.stage`, which returns 3 only when the setup is on stage 2 now AND was on stage 2 on the older trades. The 20-trade brake still drops one level (3 to 2). Any error or missing data keeps the lower stage / base.
 * **Effect:** heat limit 5 / 6 / 7 / **8 %** (`DEFAULT_MAX_PORTFOLIO_RISK_PCT` + stage x `RISK_LADDER_HEAT_STEP_PCT`). Per-trade size is untouched (still `size_ladder`). **Switch:** the existing `RISK_LADDER_ENABLED` (off = base limit); its Calibration help text now names stage 3. Stage 3 needs 300+ closed real trades with a stamp plus 60 trading days of history, so it will not appear for a long time; until then every setup behaves as in round 91.
 * **Tests:** `tests/test_e276_risk_ladder.py` gains `test_older_cells_and_stage_3` (only pre-cutoff trades counted, 60 days or fewer gives none, stage 3 and 8 %, not-sustained stays 2, no older data stays 2, older stage 2 does not lift a setup now on stage 1, brake 3 to 2). I broke it two ways on purpose (older cell ignored, cutoff off by one day) and the test failed both times.
 * **Ran here (plain runner, no pytest, SQLAlchemy or Docker):** the 4 ladder tests, `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies`, `gen_env_reference --check`, `check_merge_conflicts`, `py_compile`. **Not run:** the real `_load` against TradePosition / TradePositionEvent / TradeDecision, the Real Trade pytest suite, Docker.
**Deploy:** `docker compose up -d --build real-trade-service`. **VM check:** `bash scripts/docker_test.sh real-trade-service`.
**Still open:** removal of the shadow history views; the half-sell probe, `NO_SETUP_MODE=live` readiness, Dhan's IP rules, the admin password change.

## 1 Nov 2026 - E285 round 91: a swing take-profit level learned from the ledger, with the two columns it needs (real-trade)

**Your decision (1 Nov):** "do the swing target in the next fix". The open item said the ledger had no per-setup target data and that a rule and a column were needed. Both are added; the rule is inert until the ledger has the data, so **nothing changes on the day you deploy**.
 * **Columns (`trade_swing_ledger`, additive migration `db._ensure_swing_ledger_columns`, runs on boot like the others, Oracle `BINARY_DOUBLE`, Postgres `DOUBLE PRECISION`, both NULL):**
   - `peak_pct`: the best high the trade reached, in % over its entry price. Kept up to date at every evening scan for SHADOW rows (`engine.settle_shadow`, from the bar's high) and for OPEN LIVE rows (`live.after_scan`, from the scan row's high); never lowered (`lane.peak_pct_after`). Rows that closed before this deploy have no peak and are not counted.
   - `target_px`: the take-profit level a new LIVE entry gets when it is bought. NULL when nothing is learned yet. SHADOW rows never get one.
 * **Rule (`swing/learned_target.py`, mirror of `learned_hold.py`):** with 30+ CLOSED ledger trades that have a peak and 10+ winners (net > 0), learned % = the median of the winners' peaks; target % = max(learned %, `SWING_TARGET_MIN_R` (1.5) x the trade's own stop %, `SWING_TARGET_MIN_PCT` (3 %)); `target_px` = entry x (1 + target %). Under 30 trades or 10 winners there is no target. The ledger read is cached 10 minutes and fails to "no target".
 * **Where it acts (`live.eod_holds`, the 15:00 hold decision):** a position that `hold_verdict` would carry overnight, and whose price is at or above its `target_px`, is NOT carried: the normal square-off sells it ("target ... reached: profit taken at the close", logged as `SWING_EXIT_DECISION` and in the ledger note). It only ever turns a carry into a sale, before the carry cap. **No resting broker order, no stop change, the buy order is unchanged (`target_price=None`), no change to the SHADOW lane** (it keeps the no-target replay rules, so the numbers are not learned from their own effect). A target touched and given back inside the session is not caught (the stop and the 15:00 decision are the only watchers).
 * **Switches (Calibration tab, Swing lane, live, on the calibrator's protected list):** `SWING_LEARNED_TARGET_ENABLED` (ships `true`; OFF = no target is set and a stored one is ignored), `SWING_TARGET_MIN_R`, `SWING_TARGET_MIN_PCT`. `docs/ENV_REFERENCE.txt` regenerated; two new files added to `docs/FILE_MANIFEST.txt`.
 * **When it starts to matter:** the first targets need 30 closed trades WITH a peak, i.e. roughly the first month of evening scans after this deploy (the SHADOW lane closes several a week). Until then every swing position behaves exactly as in round 90. The 1.5 R / 3 % floors and the median are my choices, not yours or a replay's; they are the three switches above if you want them changed.
 * **Tests:** new `tests/test_e285_swing_target.py` (17: peak never lowers, target sale rules, learned value is the winners' median only, floors, cache and failure paths, the shadow lane records a peak and never a target, the order carries no target, the target applies only after `hold_verdict` says keep and before the cap, migration and switches present); `tests/test_db.py` lists `_ensure_swing_ledger_columns` in `COLUMN_FUNCS`, so its legacy-schema, idempotency and Oracle-type checks cover the new columns.
 * **Ran here (no pytest, SQLAlchemy or Docker in this sandbox; plain stand-in runner):** the 17 new tests; the 81 existing swing, learned-hold, event and catalog tests that import without a database (all pass, the one my runner cannot drive fails identically on the round 89 tree); `test_swing_lane.py` standalone; `test_autocalib` (19); `scripts/test_control_kit.py` (20); `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies`, `gen_env_reference --check`, `check_merge_conflicts`; `py_compile` on every touched file. I also broke the rule two ways on purpose (median to p75, target turning a sale into a keep) and the new tests failed both times. **Not run:** the Real Trade pytest suite, `tests/test_db.py`, the new `live.py` paths against a real database (entries, `eod_holds`, `after_scan` are checked by source-order tests and the pure helpers only), Docker.
**Deploy:** `docker compose up -d --build real-trade-service`. On boot the log should show `added trade_swing_ledger.peak_pct` and `added trade_swing_ledger.target_px`. **VM check:** `bash scripts/docker_test.sh real-trade-service` (expect the old 5838 plus the 17 new and a few `test_db` cases). **After the first evening scan:** `SELECT symbol, status, peak_pct FROM trade_swing_ledger WHERE kind='SHADOW'` should show a peak on every OPEN row.
**Still open:** the Real Trade risk ladder beyond stage 2; removal of the shadow history views; the half-sell probe, `NO_SETUP_MODE=live` readiness, Dhan's IP rules, the admin password change.

## 1 Nov 2026 - E285 round 88: Real Trade's structure gate is live at once (no log mode, no evidence downgrade)

**Decision (yours, 1 Nov):** make `enforce` truly live in Real Trade. **This changes live entries:** a bad daily structure (bearish structure, chase, stretch, no room, thin-volume breakout) now makes a REAL / DEMO entry WAIT from the first scan after the deploy. Until now `mode_for(mode, db)` quietly ran the gate as `log` (record only) whenever fewer than `STRUCTURE_ENFORCE_MIN_OOS` (15) structure observations had a measured outcome (B15), so on a young database nothing was refused.
 * `entry_engine/structure_gate.py`: `mode_for` returns `off | enforce`; the B15 downgrade and the A9 `log` value are gone (the `db` argument is kept for old callers and unused). A thin-volume breakout is always a reason when the test is on (it was an observation only under `log`).
 * `config.py`: `STRUCTURE_GATE_MODE`, `STRUCTURE_GATE_DEMO_MODE` and `STRUCTURE_BREAKOUT_VOLUME_MODE` accept `off | enforce`; a stale `log` / `shadow` / typo is `enforce`. `STRUCTURE_ENFORCE_MIN_OOS` stays only as a number the `/structure/status` route reports (`oos_observations` / `oos_needed`); it gates nothing.
 * Catalog choices, helps and `.env.example` follow. Tests: `test_b15_enforce_guard.py`, `test_a12_*`, `test_dvol_*` rewritten; `test_e273_no_shadow_modes.py` gains the guard for these three switches.
**To undo quickly:** `STRUCTURE_GATE_MODE=off` (and `STRUCTURE_GATE_DEMO_MODE=off`) in `.env`, or the Calibration page. **Deploy:** `docker compose up -d --build real-trade-service`. **Watch Monday:** how many entries the structure gate WAITs (`/structure/status`, `gate_tag "structure"` on decisions).

## 1 Nov 2026 - E285 round 87: no shadow / log values left in Position Stocks, the account-wide Dhan order window, and a readable card for /live/state

**Why:** the E284 plan's open code items (no-shadow rule in Position Stocks; "Cross-service Dhan order budget"; "Card for /live/state"). Round 86 was green on your VM; this round only adds the three items below. Tested here with the real pytest suites (Position Stocks full suite and the touched Real Trade files), the static checks and the frontend `node` tests; **not** through Docker, so re-run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh position-stocks-service` and `real-trade-service` on the VM.
 * **No-shadow rule, Position Stocks (`config.py`, `orders/structure_gate.py`, `orders/trade_odds.py`, `control/catalog.py`, `.env.example`).**
   - `SHORT_MODE` is `off | live`. A leftover `shadow` reads as `off` (a shadow never ordered, so a leftover must not start ordering).
   - `TRADE_ODDS_MODE` is `live | off` and ships `live`. A leftover `shadow` or a typo reads as `live`; only `off` stops the odds record. It stays informational: nothing reads it to decide a trade.
   - `STRUCTURE_GATE_MODE` and `STRUCTURE_BREAKOUT_VOLUME_MODE` are `enforce | off`. The E72 `log` value is gone under the `PS_` names too; a stale `log` / `shadow` / typo is `enforce`. A thin-volume breakout is now always a refusal reason when the test is on (it was an observation only under `log`).
   - Catalog choices, helps and `shipped` values follow; `docs/ENV_REFERENCE.txt` regenerated.
   - New guard `tests/test_e285_no_shadow_modes.py` (Real Trade has had its own since E273). `test_e72_*` and `test_short_foundation.py` rewritten for the new values.
   - **Not removed:** the dead shadow-short recorder (`orders/short_entry.attempt_short_shadow`, `/short/shadow`, `ShortShadowCard`) stays; config can no longer reach it. See the remaining list.
 * **Account-wide Dhan order window (`execution/shared_rate_window.py`, identical copy in both services; `execution/order_rate.py`; Real Trade `execution/dhan_client.py`).** E282's per-second budget counted only this process's calls. Now both services append every Dhan order call (place, cancel, resting stop, resting stop cancel, Forever order and its cancel) to one small JSON file on a new `shared-runtime` Docker volume (`/data/shared/order_window.json`, an `flock` guards it). A Position Stocks stop / target **modify** waits while the account-wide count in the last second is at `ORDER_RATE_ACCOUNT_PER_S` (default 8 of Dhan's 10; 2 hard cap seconds as before); entries, cancels and exits are never delayed; Real Trade only records and never waits. Fail open everywhere: switch off (`ORDER_RATE_SHARED_ENABLED`, in both services' catalogs, live), no file system, lock held over ~50 ms or a corrupt file all fall back to the per-process window. The file keeps at most 200 entries. `docker-compose.yml` mounts the volume in both services; `check_shared_copies.py` pins the two copies identical. `/live/state` shows the window (both services).
 * **Card for `/live/state` (`frontend/src/liveStateView.ts`, `StatusProbesPanel.tsx`).** The probe now shows readable blocks above the raw JSON (kept under a "Raw JSON" fold): Real Trade = pool rupees, heat, newest entries by setup, Dhan order window; Position Stocks = order calls and waits, the shared window, momentum exit, the other service's heat, ladder stage per setup. A failed part is one red row. `liveStateView.test.mjs` (in the `npm test` chain) pins the field names against both services' `live_state.py`.
 * **Operations note (VM, 1 Nov 2026, from the last session):** `GATEWAY_CORS_ORIGINS` was set in `.env` to `https://stockky.duckdns.org,http://stockky.duckdns.org,http://130.210.2.159,http://130.210.2.159:5173` and the gateway restarted; `curl -H "Origin: https://stockky.duckdns.org" .../api/health` answered `access-control-allow-origin: https://stockky.duckdns.org`. Nothing in the code changed for this.

**Deploy:** `docker compose up -d --build position-stocks-service real-trade-service` (the new volume is created on the first `up`). Then open `/live/state` in the Calibration tab on both services: the Dhan order window should show `file usable: on`.
**Not changed:** no trading rule, no entry or exit threshold, no database column.

## 1 Nov 2026 - E284 round 85: the Docker failures of rounds 75 to 84, fixed (real-trade, position-stocks)

**Why:** the VM ran both Docker suites on the round 84 tree: Real Trade 10 failed / 5828 passed, Position Stocks 26 failed / 5093 passed. pytest and the service dependencies now install in the build sandbox, so every failure was reproduced and read from its traceback. After the fixes below both full suites pass here: **Position Stocks 5120 passed, Real Trade 5837 passed and 2 skipped**. The analysis-intelligence suite was 2667 passed on the VM (round 84 tree); not re-run here (no `feedparser` / `yfinance` in the sandbox), only its changed file (`test_e280_event_feed`, 20).
**Real code defects (3):**
 * `real-trade-service/entry_engine/risk_stage.py` filtered `broker_imported.is_(False)`, which Oracle renders `IS 0` (ORA-00908). The closed-trade read behind the Real Trade risk ladder would have failed (and the ladder stayed at stage 0). Now `== False`; `tests/test_oracle_boolean_filters.py` caught it.
 * `real-trade-service/swing/live.py` `overdue()` (my round 81): a position with no database session (a plain object, a detached row) made the learned-hold lookup raise, so the 5-session backstop returned "no sale". New `_session_of()` falls back to the constant hold.
 * `orders/volume_profile.py` (my round 83): it skipped every file marked `backfilled`, which on the VM was most complete days (snapshot showed 1 day / 250 symbol-days of 9). ## 1 Nov 2026 - E284 round 84: a news feed that only returns old items is stale, not healthy (analysis-intelligence)

**Found (VM `check_event_sources.py`):** Moneycontrol and Moneycontrol-business still answer HTTP 200 but their newest item is about 2.5 years old. `FeedCollector.poll_once` dropped the old items but still marked the source `ok: true` (it had entries), so the typed event feed counted two dead URLs as healthy sources and polled them every cycle. With the four live sources up this hid nothing today; if the Google and LiveMint sources failed, the feed would still have reported `ok: true` with no events.
**Fixed:** `event/event_feed.py`: a source whose readable items are ALL older than `EVENT_FEED_MAX_AGE_H` (24) is reported `ok: false`, status `stale`, and left alone for `EVENT_FEED_BLOCK_COOLDOWN_S` (1800 s), like a blocked source. A source with at least one fresh item, an empty source and a blocked source behave as before. A weekend with only old items makes every source `stale` and the feed `ok: false`, which is the true statement (no fresh news); nothing blocks a trade by it (unchanged rule). The 403 source (NDTVProfit) was already cooled down for 30 minutes. No source was removed from `FEEDS`: a URL that recovers is picked up again.
**Tests:** `tests/test_e280_event_feed.py` +1 (a 900-day-old feed is not healthy, is polled once in two cycles while the live one is polled twice, and an all-stale collector answers `ok: false` with no events); the file's 20 tests pass with the plain runner. `test_event_main`, `test_group240_event_shared_feed_fetch` and `test_group213_event_keyword_matching` import pytest and did not run here: run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh analysis-intelligence-service`. **VM check:** the typed event feed route (`/events/<SYMBOL>/active`, with any symbol) lists them under `sources`; it shows `Moneycontrol` and `Moneycontrol-business` as `stale` and `healthy_sources` has only the live ones (I did not check the exact route path here).

## 1 Nov 2026 - E284 round 83: the normal volume pace learned from the recorded bars (position-stocks)

**What:** the RVOL half of `ADAPTIVE_ENTRY_PARAMS` (plan E282 item 2). The liquidity gate's relative-volume test (`LIQUIDITY_RVOL`, and the size penalty built on it) compared today's volume with `normal daily volume x share of the session elapsed`, and that share was a guessed curve, `(minutes / 375) ** 0.6`. New `orders/volume_profile.py` measures it: for every COMPLETE recorded day (starts within 5 minutes of 09:15, 80 % of the minutes present, not backfilled, no index files) and symbol it takes the cumulative share of the day's volume by each minute, and the curve is the median over all of them (made non-decreasing). `liquidity_gate.elapsed_fraction` passes the old value through `volume_profile.adjust`.
**Guard rails:** needs 5+ complete recorded days AND 300+ symbol-days, else the old curve unchanged; shrunk toward the old curve with n / (n + 300); the result stays within +-25 % of the old curve at that minute (a hard bound) and never above 1; built once in a background thread and cached 6 hours, so the entry path never waits for the disk; any error, an unready build or the switch OFF gives the old value. `ENTRY_MIN_RVOL`, the 09:45 start and every other liquidity test are untouched. Switch `VOLUME_PROFILE_LEARNED_ENABLED` (ships on, inert until the data exists) in the Calibration tab under Entry guards and on the calibrator's protected list; `tests/conftest.py` turns it off for older tests.
**Limits:** the sample is the symbols the recorder keeps (top by turnover plus scan candidates), market-wide rather than per stock, and about 9 days at most today; the old curve front-loads about 22 % by 09:45, so the 25 % band is what decides how far this can move at first. Not built: a per-stock own-minute percentile, the VWAP-tolerance and spread percentiles.
**Tests:** `tests/test_e284_volume_profile.py` (7: straight curve from flat volume, too few days or symbol-days gives no curve, late or thin files excluded, shrink and the +-25 % band, off / not ready / broken gives the old value, the gate uses it, switch in config and catalog). Ran here with a plain stand-in runner: those 7, `test_e273_rvol_penalty` 4, `test_e37_catalog_coverage` 3, `test_group477_catalog_matches_config`, `test_e283_long_target` 13, calibration-runner `test_autocalib` 19, `check_control_catalog.py`, `check_shared_copies.py`, `gen_env_reference.py --check` (1,754 variables). **Did not run:** pytest and the Docker suites; `test_group333_liquidity_gate` imports pytest so it did not run here (its `elapsed_fraction` assertions are unchanged because the profile is off or empty in tests).
**VM check:** after the next full session of recording, `docker compose exec -T position-stocks-service python -c "from orders import volume_profile as v; v.adjust(0.2, 30); import time; time.sleep(5); print(v.snapshot())"` should show `ready: True` with days >= 5.

## 1 Nov 2026 - E282 round 77: a per-second budget for Dhan order calls (position-stocks)

**Found:** nothing counted Dhan order calls per second. Dhan's order API allows 10 a second for the whole account (secondary sources; verify on Dhan's own page), shared with real-trade-service. The existing `shared_order_budget` is a DAILY cap only. A market-wide drop can make the stop and target modifies of every open position (trailing, breakeven, tape, momentum, event and reconcile paths, each with its own per-position timer) land in the same second.
**Fixed:** new `execution/order_rate.py` (position-stocks only), called from `execution/dhan_client.py` right before every broker order call (place, cancel, super-order place / modify / cancel, convert: 11 sites). One rolling one-second window, `ORDER_RATE_PER_S` calls (default 4, so this service leaves 6 a second for real-trade-service). Only a stop / target MODIFY (`modify_super_order`) waits when the window is full: one caller waits at most one second (the oldest call leaves the window by then), with a hard cap of 2 s after which the call goes through anyway, so a busy window slows a modify and never starves it. Every other order call (entry, cancel, exit, convert) is counted and NEVER delayed, so an exit or a protective stop does not wait behind a trailing move; counted calls use up the budget, so modifies yield to them. Fails open (any error = no wait). `ORDER_RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED` (live, ships on) and `ORDER_RATE_PER_S` in the Calibration tab under Entry guards; both on the calibrator's protected list; `docs/ENV_REFERENCE.txt` regenerated. `tests/conftest.py` turns the limiter off by default so older tests never sleep.
**Worst case (the plan's "order-call budget test"):** 20 modifies (10 positions x stop + target) in the same instant are admitted four a second over 4 seconds, no call waits more than 1 s, and no sliding second holds more than 4 (test below). Real-trade-service is NOT changed: its broker-stop calls are cancel + place pairs that protect an open position and are never delayed; the 6 a second left for it covers one sync pass of 5 positions.
**Not done / limits:** nothing coordinates the two services' counts (each process has its own window, the split is by configuration); the data API (5 a second), the quote API (1 a second) and read-only calls such as `get_order_list` are not counted; `verify_stop_leg` read-backs are reads. No UI route shows the counters yet (`order_rate.snapshot()` exists for one).
**Tests:** `tests/test_e282_order_rate.py` (11, a fake clock): under budget never waits, 5th modify waits for the window, the 20-modify worst case, other calls counted never delayed, a modify yields to counted calls, the 2 s cap, switch off, config bounds (1..9), broken clock fails open, every order call in the client is preceded by a count and only `modify_super_order` waits, the switches are on the Calibration page and protected.
**Ran here (plain stand-in runner; `pytest`, `sqlalchemy`, `httpx` are not installable in the build sandbox):** the 11 new tests, the 28 round-76 tests, `test_e37_catalog_coverage` 3, `test_e272_setup_stamp_stats` 8, `test_autocalib` 19, `test_regime_shadow_report` 8, `check_shared_copies.py`, `check_control_catalog.py`, `gen_env_reference.py --check`, byte-compile: all pass. **Did not run:** the Docker suites, in particular the existing `dhan_client` and exit / trailing tests that now pass through `order_rate` (the conftest switch keeps them from sleeping).

## 1 Nov 2026 - E282 round 76: a stop learned from the setup's own winners (position-stocks)

**What:** the first half of `ADAPTIVE_ENTRY_PARAMS` (plan E282 item 2, the item the plan rates highest). A stop used to depend only on the stock's own noise, the same for every setup. A setup's closed WINNERS record how far they went against the entry before paying (`min_price_seen` for a long, `max_price_seen` for a short). New `orders/learned_stop.py` takes the 90th percentile of that distance for the setup's cell, adds 10 %, holds it to +-25 % a week (`learned_limit`), shrinks it toward the plan's own stop with `n / (n + 30)`, and uses it as the stop. Long (S1 / S2 / S3) in `orders/entry.py`, short (S4 / S2 / S3) in `orders/short_entry.py`; the note is stamped in `flow_json["learned_stop"]` (record only; the short side through `short_live.py`).
**Guard rails (plan, "Guard rails for every learned value"):** a cell with fewer than 30 closed trades or fewer than 10 winners with a readable excursion gives the plan's stop unchanged; the stop only ever gets TIGHTER (never wider than the plan's), a long's rounded down to the tick and a short's up so the rounded stop is never tighter than computed; never below the stock's own noise floor (the largest of `LEARNED_STOP_MIN_PCT` 0.5 %, `OPENING_GATE_MIN_STOP_ATR_FRAC` x daily ATR and the E251 volatility floor), and with no daily ATR, or when that floor is on and cannot be read, the stop is left alone; the legacy shortlist cell never learns (it mixes long and short entries); the target, the 2:1 floor and the SIZE are untouched: the position is still sized from the plan's stop (`_budget_stop_pct`), so a tighter stop lowers the risk and never grows the position. The Time Machine and cost gates judge the levels AFTER this.
**Also:** `setup_stats.summarize` cells gain `mae_n` and `adverse_p90_pct` (winners only; None under 10 samples; a winner that never went against the entry counts as 0). Switches `LEARNED_STOP_ENABLED` (live, ships on: it does nothing until a cell has 30 trades) and `LEARNED_STOP_MIN_PCT` in the Calibration tab under Entry guards; both are on the calibrator's protected list (`autocalib.py`); `docs/ENV_REFERENCE.txt` regenerated.
**Expect:** most stops will not move at first. The E251 floor already puts many plan stops near the stock's noise, and a learned stop never goes below it. The note shows up on a trade only when the stop really was tightened.
**Tests:** `tests/test_e282_learned_stop.py` (28: winners-only excursion, 10-winner minimum, short reads the high, cell choice and fall-back to ALL, legacy never learns, short S2 own cell, shrinkage, weekly limit, tighter-only, tick rounding both ways, noise floor, configured minimum, unreadable floor fails closed, unknown ATR, switch off, wrong-side stop, short mirror, stamp, the wiring read as text: size from the plan stop, the setup known before the call, the Time Machine after it).
**Ran here (plain stand-in runner; `pytest`, `sqlalchemy` and `httpx` are not installable in the build sandbox):** the 28 new tests, `test_e37_catalog_coverage` 3, `test_e272_setup_stamp_stats` 8, `test_autocalib` 19, `test_regime_shadow_report` 8, `check_shared_copies.py`, `check_control_catalog.py`, `gen_env_reference.py --check`, byte-compile of every changed file: all pass. **Did not run:** the Docker suites, `tests/test_e274_short_target.py` and the other database-backed suites. The noise-floor test uses a stand-in for `orders/adaptive.stop_floor_pct` only when the real module cannot be imported (as in the build sandbox); on the VM it uses the real one. Five `test_e275_*` tests fail in the sandbox with or without this change (they need `sqlalchemy`).
**Not done:** the RVOL-percentile half of `ADAPTIVE_ENTRY_PARAMS`, a learned long target, the VWAP and spread percentiles; Real Trade is untouched (swing trades have no setup cell with closed-trade history yet).

## 1 Nov 2026 - E278 round 75: sweep study (offline script, no trading effect)

**What:** new `scripts/sweep_study.py` (+ `scripts/test_sweep_study.py`, 24 tests, wired into `.github/workflows/service-tests.yml` beside `ma_baseline`). It answers the one question the Liquidity Lab material leaves open: after a liquidity sweep, does price reverse, continue, or neither, on NSE data. It is an offline study like `ma_baseline.py` and `preopen_momentum_study.py`: it imports no service, reads no env, places nothing, adds no switch and no database column. Not a shadow mode.
**Direction-agnostic:** every event is scored as a long and as a short, with a day-clustered 90 % bootstrap (all stocks of a day share the market's move) and a matched baseline. A side counts as an edge only when BOTH intervals (net after costs, and excess over the baseline) are above zero. Fewer than 30 events per direction (or fewer than 20 day clusters) prints counts only: `insufficient_sample`, nothing else.
**Parts:** A daily (`sweep_low` / `sweep_high` = wick through the previous day's low / high by at least 0.10 ATR14 and a close back inside; `break_high` / `break_low` = a close beyond it, the second ICT bias rule). Entry = next open, exit = close of day i+h for h = 1 (the next session), 3, 5; cost 0.30 % + 0.10 % slippage; baseline = the same stock's non-event days of the same month; raw and market-adjusted views. B the full 3-step pattern on recorded 1-minute bars (daily bias, 30-minute sweep with rejection, 5-minute displacement of 1.5 x ATR and 60 % body within 3 bars, order block within 5 bars before it): COUNTS ONLY, never an edge verdict. C failed opening-range break (09:15-09:19 range; first closed break before 09:55; a close back inside within 3 bars = failed, otherwise held; returns to 10:00 / 10:30 / 15:00 at 0.15 % cost; baseline = held breaks).
**Pre-registered:** one sweep depth (first of `--depths`, default 0.10), one displacement size, one reclaim window, one primary horizon (h = 1; 10:00). Extra values are reported but never used for a verdict; every variant is counted in `variants_tried`.
**Not run here:** nothing on real data (no history folder or `bar-records` in the build sandbox, no network); the 24 tests use fixed synthetic bars and pass under the plain runner (`python3 scripts/test_sweep_study.py`). `pytest` is not installable here, so the CI line is untested.
**VM step:** `python3 scripts/fetch_history_for_backtest.py ...` (daily history for the 744-name swing universe, same loader as `ma_baseline.py`), copy the `bar-records` volume to the host, then `python3 scripts/sweep_study.py --daily HIST_DIR --bars BAR_DIR --out OUT_DIR`. Read the verdict per kind; the plan's table (section 4, E278) says what ships for each outcome. E279 to E281 are NOT built: they exist only if this study supports a direction.

New `design-v8.css` (loads after v7): one-time rail sweep, soft rise of tiles and cards, one ring on the running slot; all off under reduced-motion, TV mode and Animations: Off. **Old labels removed:** the Swing lane is live or off only (a leftover `shadow` value reads as off, as the service does): `swingView`, `SwingPanel`, a two-rung `ModeLadder` (`twoStep`), `calibrationPlan` text ("SWING_MODE stays shadow" gone), Watchlist tab and the Board comment. **Backend:** read-only admin `GET /premarket/plan` (position-stocks `main.py`). **Tests:** `sessionView.test.mjs`, `design-v8.test.mjs` (both in `npm test`; whole chain passes), `swing.test.mjs` updated, `services/position-stocks-service/tests/test_e277_premarket_route.py`. **Not run:** `tsc` / `vite build` (no network in the build sandbox, so node_modules could not be installed: run `npm ci && npx tsc --noEmit && npm run build`), the full pytest suites, a browser. **Not built:** routes for risk-ladder stage, shared heat and momentum-exit state, and a setup stamp on Real Trade positions (no backend source for them yet). `SHORT_MODE` keeps its shadow value in the service, so its card still says shadow when set to shadow.

# Stockky v2 - Changelog

One file for the whole history, newest first. Add **one bullet per release** at the top of the first list: `- E<number> (<date>): what changed and why, switches/env names,
tests that ran and tests that did not`. Do not create per-release `CHANGES_*.md` files or other Markdown notes: the repo keeps only `README.md` and this file.

Release notes, audit reports, service sheets and session notes that used to be separate files (about 790 `.md` files) were folded into this changelog on 2026-10-28
(E245) and removed from the working tree. File names quoted inside older entries refer to those removed files; they are in git history
(`git log --diff-filter=D --name-only -- "docs/*.md"`, then `git show <commit>^:docs/<file>.md`).

## Releases (E-numbered, newest first)

- **E274 round 44 (31 Oct 2026): setups S2 and S3 can match a short (position-stocks-service).** **Found:** `setup_engine` already knows S2 (weak stock of the day breaking its opening range) and S3 (exhaustion after a spike) for a short, but nothing fed it short facts, so `setup_short.decide` only ever looked at S4 and returned at once without a negative typed event. **Fixed:** `orders/setup_inputs.py` gets the short mirrors (`gather_short`, `s2_facts_short`, `s3_facts_short`, `pullback_hold_short`, `structure_ok_short`, `tape_exhaustion_short`, `wall_close_short`; `spike_z` takes an optional direction): range-low break or rally-back-and-fail, bearish candle, volume, up-spike beyond the stock's own volatility, shooting-star failure at the high, buying exhaustion / absorption on the reliable tape, price stretched above VWAP, climax volume, resistance wall within 0.3 %. `setup_short.decide` now also allows S2 / S3 (switch `SETUP_S2_S3_SHORT_ENABLED`, behind `SETUP_S2_ENABLED` / `SETUP_S3_ENABLED`), reads the facts once and stamps the match with side SHORT; without a negative event only S2 / S3 can fire. **Own cells:** a short S2 / S3 is keyed `S2_RS_BREAKOUT_SHORT|slot` (`setup_stats.cell_id` / `cell_key`), never mixed with the long cell, in `summarize`, the slot ladder (promotion / demotion), the learned time stop and the momentum exit; long cells and old stamps are unchanged, and the round 43 learned short target may now read these cells. **Kept narrow:** only an S4 short earns the 09:21 early window, the Board-gate skip and the squeeze-guard exemption; an S2 / S3 short is sized by the engine (half, quarter in the 09:20 slot), and every other gate runs as before. `setup_engine.py` is untouched (shared copy with real-trade-service stays identical). Tests: `tests/test_e274_short_s2_s3.py` (20).
- **E274 round 45 (31 Oct 2026): a fresh negative event on a stock Real Trade holds sells it or tightens its stop (real-trade-service).** **Found:** the plan says S4 is the exit trigger for a held stock in Real Trade (long only), but nothing checked open positions against the typed event feed: the negative-event veto only stopped new longs. **Fixed:** new `exit_engine/event_exit.py` (pure) and `intraday_news.negative_event(db, symbol, opened_at)` (today's market-hours snapshot, only an event announced after the entry; or the after-hours / pre-open veto row, only for a position opened on an earlier day; cached 60 s, fail-open). Exit loop step 3c: strength >= 0.60 sells (reason `event_exit`, any P&L), 0.40 to 0.60 raises the stop to just under the price (0.25 x ATR, or 0.5 % without ATR, rounded down to the tick, never lowered); never before 09:20. Switches: `EVENT_EXIT_MODE` (off | tighten | exit, default exit), `EVENT_EXIT_MIN_STRENGTH`, `EVENT_EXIT_STRONG_STRENGTH`, `EVENT_EXIT_TIGHT_ATR`, `EVENT_EXIT_START_IST`, `EVENT_EXIT_MAX_AGE_MINUTES`; three in the control catalog. Sell uses the existing `_send_real_sell` (market). Not done: limit-order exit at 09:20 for an after-hours event and a positive event extending the time stop. Tests: `tests/test_e274_event_exit.py` (9, run).
- **E274 round 46 (31 Oct 2026): the exit for after-hours news on a held stock is a limit sell, not a market sell (real-trade-service).** **Found:** round 45 sold a held stock at MARKET on a strong negative event; the plan says a limit order at 09:20 for news that came after hours, so the gap open is not sold into a thin book. **Fixed:** `event_exit.limit_price_for` gives last price minus `EVENT_EXIT_LIMIT_BUFFER_PCT` (default 0.5 %), rounded DOWN to the tick, only when the event source is the after-hours / pre-open row; the exit loop sends it as a LIMIT `event_exit` sell through the existing `_send_real_sell`. A market-hours event stays MARKET. Safety: the existing exit-limit expiry pass cancels an unfilled one after `EXIT_LIMIT_VALIDITY_MINUTES` (3) and resends the remainder as MARKET; a new `_event_exit_order_pending` stops a second sell while the first is working. Switch `EVENT_EXIT_LIMIT_ENABLED` (default on; off = always MARKET), in the control catalog. Tests: `tests/test_e274_event_exit.py` (10, run). Still not done: a positive event extending the time stop.
- **E274 round 47 (31 Oct 2026): Real Trade's morning swing pass buys event-backed picks first and refuses a pick with a fresh negative event (real-trade-service).** **Found:** the swing entry (limit order from 09:21, open-reversal skip, gap and sector checks) already existed, but it ranked picks by scan rank only and never looked at the typed event feed. **Fixed:** `intraday_news.positive_event_any` (today's market-hours positive event, else the after-hours / pre-open row tagged p=+1; fail-open) and `swing/lane.order_event_first` (backed picks first, stronger event first, scan rank inside each group; pure) order the day's picks in `swing/live.entries`; the same pass now calls `event_veto_reason` per pick and skips with `EVENT_VETO: ...` (it did not before for the swing lane). The entry Telegram message shows the event type and strength. Switches `SWING_EVENT_FIRST_ENABLED` and `SWING_EVENT_VETO_ENABLED` (default on, in the control catalog). Tests: `tests/test_e274_swing_event_first.py` (4, run). Not run here: the live `entries` path (needs the real stack). Still not done in Real Trade: a positive event extending the time stop, results-date handling against the hold window, 5-minute momentum exits, setup-based time stops, heat with a gap allowance.
- **E274 round 48 (31 Oct 2026): a swing position in profit is checked for fading momentum on 5-minute bars during the session (real-trade-service).** **Found:** `exit_engine/momentum_health.py` (shared, identical copy) was only wired in Position Stocks; Real Trade's swing lane exited on its stop, the 15:00 re-check and the structure rule only. **Fixed:** new `exit_engine/swing_momentum.py` rolls today's cached 1-minute bars (`orb_bars`, never waits for the network) into complete 5-minute bars from the later of the entry and 09:15, hands them to `momentum_health.decide` (with the forming bar, because `decide` ignores the last row) and returns HOLD / TIGHTEN / BOOK. Real Trade has no tape and no VWAP-fade feed, so the signals that can fire are no_new_best (90 min without a new high), climax_bar and at_wall; 2 signals raise the stop (never lowered), 3 signals plus 1 % profit sell (`momentum_exit`, market). Exit loop step 3d: swing positions only, in profit, 09:45 to 15:00 IST, once a minute per position, at least 6 complete bars, stale bars = no opinion. Switches: `SWING_MOMENTUM_EXIT_MODE` (off | tighten | exit, default exit, in the control catalog), `SWING_MOMENTUM_BOOK_N`, `_MIN_PROFIT_PCT`, `_MIN_BARS`, `_NO_NEW_BEST_MIN`, `_GAP_MIN_PCT`. Tests: `tests/test_e274_swing_momentum.py` (7, run). Not done: the no_new_best time and typical gain from the setup's own history (fixed 90 min for now), a Real Trade tape signal, a positive event extending the time stop, heat with a gap allowance.
- **E276 round 72 (1 Nov 2026): the setup replay is a runner job with off-only calibrator rules; the deprecated `utcfromtimestamp` is gone (calibration-runner, real-trade, position-stocks, decision-prediction).** `setup_backtest` (`scripts/setup_backtest.py`, built in E276) was a script nobody could run from the runner: it is now a heavy, closed-market job (params: last N days, hold minutes, round-trip cost) and a row of the monthly evidence pack, after `intraday_gates`. `autocalib.r_setup` gives `SETUP_S2_ENABLED` and `SETUP_S3_ENABLED` (position-stocks only) an evidence rule: a `no_edge` verdict (the whole day-clustered 90 % interval below zero) on S2_RS_BREAKOUT_LONG / S3_EXHAUSTION_LONG, confirmed by two different runs, switches that setup OFF; any other verdict, a missing file or a setup already off changes nothing, and a report never switches a setup on. No rule for Real Trade: the replay runs the intraday engine on 1-minute bars and says nothing about 2 to 5 day holds. S1 / S4 get no rule (bars hold no typed events, so they report n = 0). `datetime.utcfromtimestamp(x)` (deprecated in Python 3.12) became `datetime.fromtimestamp(x, timezone.utc)` in `regime/data.py`, both `candidate_outcomes.py` copies and the two decision-prediction training scripts: same value, no warning. `scripts/no_setup_readiness.py` and the weekly runner job `no_setup_readiness` apply the plan's rule for `NO_SETUP_MODE=live` to position-stocks' `/setup/stats` (10 sessions, 30 stamped entries, matched average R at least the shortlist's, under half unmatched); read-only, it never sets the switch, exit 1 = NOT_READY. `setup_stats` cells gained a `days` field (sessions with a stamped trade) for it; sessions are a lower bound (largest cell). Decided without new evidence: `NO_SETUP_MODE` stays off and the half-sell stays unbuilt (the Dhan probe is the gate); nothing to change for the BE-series swing names (E273 round 14 already skips them in the evening scan) or the bar-records mount (already in compose). **Tests that ran (plain-python runner, pytest not installable here):** `test_e276_no_setup_readiness` 4, `test_e272_setup_stamp_stats` 8, `test_autocalib` 19 (3 new: job + pack row, off-only rule, rule reads what `summarize()` really writes), the whole calibration-runner folder 268 passing, 0 failing, `test_e272_candidate_outcomes` 7 (PS) and 8 (RT), `check_shared_copies`, `check_control_catalog`, `check_merge_conflicts`. **Did not run:** the full pytest suite (three setup_stats test files import pytest), the VM suite, a real `setup_backtest` run on recorded bars.
- **E276 round 71 (1 Nov 2026): the calibrator's protected list covers the six new short-selling switches (calibration-runner).** `test_regime_shadow_report.py::test_every_structure_and_market_state_key_is_either_a_rule_or_protected` failed because `SHORT_DAY_FALL_RANGE_MULT`, `SHORT_DAY_FALL_HARD_CEILING_PCT` (round 67), `SHORT_SQUEEZE_GUARD_ENABLED`, `SHORT_TARGET_HVN_ENABLED`, `SHORT_TARGET_LEARNED_ENABLED` and `SHORT_TARGET_MIN_PCT` (E274) are in the catalog but were neither a Rule nor in `autocalib.PROTECTED`. They are now protected, like every other `SHORT_*` threshold: no test measures them, so the weekly calibrator never moves them. Only `autocalib.py` changed; no runtime service code. **Tests that ran (plain-python runner, pytest not installable here):** `test_regime_shadow_report` 8, `test_autocalib` 16, `test_e115_tm_gate_trigger` 8, `test_short_shadow_report` 8, `test_regime_package` 30, all passing. **Did not run:** the full pytest suite and the VM suite.
- **E276 round 70 (1 Nov 2026): Real Trade gets the "no setup, no trade" refusal, shipped off (real-trade).** `NO_SETUP_MODE` (off | live, catalog enum, ships off) now exists in Real Trade as in Position Stocks. `setup_live.no_setup_refusal` refuses only when the mode is live, the walk ran with S1, S2 and S3 all able to fire (all three switches on and the clock at or past 09:45, so a 09:30 candidate is never refused for a setup that cannot fire yet), the engine returned a real NO_SETUP (not "no result", not an engine error, not PENALTY_TOO_HIGH) and the entry is not a pullback re-arm. The refusal is a WAIT with gate_tag `no_setup`, so the candidate is looked at again next cycle. Env reference unchanged at 1,744 (the variable was already read by Position Stocks). **Tests that ran:** `tests/test_e276_rt_setups_s2_s3.py` now 16 (the refusal rules incl. a real-engine NO_SETUP), the RT S1 / gap-event / no-shadow runners, `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies`, `gen_env_reference`, `check_merge_conflicts`. **Did not run:** pytest-only files and the VM suite.
- **E276 round 69 (1 Nov 2026): swing lane caps raised together (real-trade).** `SWING_MAX_POSITIONS` 2 -> 3 and `SWING_PICKS_PER_DAY` 2 -> 3, the number the plan left open ("raise them together" when the general cap went 3 -> 5). Chosen as a step, not a jump: the lane has never bought, so 3 / 3 scales the general cap's increase without matching it, and the guards that matter still bind (`SWING_MAX_EXPOSURE_PCT` 40 %, `SWING_MAX_PER_SECTOR` 1, the shared heat limit, `SWING_BACKUP_PICKS` 1). Code fallbacks in `swing/live.py` / `swing/engine.py`, catalog `shipped`, `.env.example` and two default-asserting tests follow. An explicit value in the VM .env still wins. **Not built, by decision:** the half-sell (needs the live Dhan probe, protocol written in the plan) and `NO_SETUP_MODE` (stays off until the evidence rule in the plan is met). **Tests that ran:** the six swing plain runners and the new RT S2 / S3 test, `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies`, `gen_env_reference`.
- **E276 round 68 (1 Nov 2026): Real Trade runs setups S2 and S3-long, not only S1 (real-trade).** Until now RT's setup walk fired S1 only (no relative-strength rank, z-scores or tape). New `entry_engine/setup_inputs.py` holds the same measuring functions Position Stocks uses (candle, volume vs norm, pullback hold, spike z-score, rs_pct, sector third, structure_ok, priced_in) plus an RT `gather_long` that is pure: `entry.py` passes it the closed 1-minute bars, the Market Board body, the daily structure summary, the book imbalance and the flow tape. `board_client` now keeps the whole board body (`get_payload()`, same cache and TTL as `get_rows`). `setup_live.facts_long` takes `inputs` and `allowed_set()` (as in PS); `entry.py` runs the walk when S1 has a typed event, or, from 09:30 (S2) / 09:45 (S3), for any candidate (non-event candidates read cached bars only, never a blocking fetch). A match is stamped with its id and sized by the engine (S2 half size, S3 probation). **RT differences from PS:** `tape_exhaustion` = the reliable flow tape, else a bid-heavy 5-level book (above +0.15; weaker evidence, the same stand-in S1 uses); `liquid` = normal daily turnover at least `SETUP_S3_LIQUID_MIN_TURNOVER_CR` (100 crore, a starting value, because RT has no all-market turnover rank); `wall_close` is normally false (the RT tape stamp carries no support node). S1 now also gets `sector_top_third` and the priced-in refusal in RT. New switches `SETUP_S2_ENABLED`, `SETUP_S3_ENABLED` (live, true) and `SETUP_S3_LIQUID_MIN_TURNOVER_CR`. `NO_SETUP` still does not refuse in RT. Catalog: 2 flags. Env reference 1,744 variables. **Tests that ran:** new `tests/test_e276_rt_setups_s2_s3.py` (15, plain runner; the board_client check skips without httpx), the RT E272 to E276 plain runners, `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies`, `check_merge_conflicts`, `gen_env_reference --check`. **Did not run:** pytest-only files, `bash scripts/docker_test.sh real-trade-service` and `position-stocks-service` on the VM. Not exercised anywhere: the live `entry.py` block itself (it needs the full service); it is covered by a source-shape check and by the pure chain test.

- **E276 round 61 (1 Nov 2026): the heat decision, made - the stage 0 total open-risk limit stays at 5 % in both services; no code change, comments and plan updated.** The plan asked to choose between raising the stage 0 heat limit (6 to 8 %) and keeping it and accepting smaller per-trade risk at five positions. **Kept at 5 %.** Reasons: (1) the live base is already 5 % (`DEFAULT_MAX_PORTFOLIO_RISK_PCT` in Real Trade, `HEAT_LIMIT_PCT` in Position Stocks), not the 4 % the plan assumed, so stage 0 is not tighter than today and the plan's 'never lower than today' rule holds; (2) 5 to 6 % of equity is the usual ceiling on total open risk (Elder's 6 % rule, the 5 to 6 % range Van Tharp's heat teaching works in), so 5 % is at the conservative end of the convention and 6 to 8 % would be above it before any edge is shown; (3) the edge is unproven (entry source -0.11 % net at 60 minutes, realized P&L about -4.5 % of the pool), and raising the ceiling only raises how fast a bad run drains the pool; (4) the daily-loss cap (3 % Real Trade, 4 % Position Stocks) sits below the heat limit, so a base above 5 % would let open risk exceed what one bad day is allowed to lose. **What 5 % means on the Rs 7,288 pool:** about Rs 364, so two full 2 % trades (about Rs 146 each) and a half; the five slots fill only when conviction scales risk down to about 1 % a trade. The ladder still raises the limit by 1 point per stage with evidence (6 % at stage 1, 7 % at stage 2, 8 % at stage 3). **Half-Kelly at stage 3 is NOT built, on purpose:** the 3 % per-trade ceiling is below half-Kelly for any realistic edge on this pool, so the ceiling always binds and the formula would change nothing. Plan item 'Real Trade 3 to 5 positions' was already done in E273 round 22 (`DEFAULT_MAX_CONCURRENT_POSITIONS` = 5); the swing lane keeps SWING_MAX_POSITIONS 2 and SWING_PICKS_PER_DAY 2.
- **E276 round 60 (1 Nov 2026): the same-sector rule - a new entry in a sector that already holds an open position counts 1.5x in the shared heat check (position-stocks, real-trade-service).** `portfolio_heat.py` (identical copy in both) gets `sector_key`, `sector_surcharge` (0.5 x (that sector's open risk + the new risk), 0 when nothing risky is open there) and `merge_sector_risk`, and `check()` takes an optional `sector_extra` (default 0 = the old maths). The sector comes from the existing symbol map (`feed/sector_map.py`, copied to Real Trade as `risk_engine/sector_map.py` and added to the identical-copy check). **A long and a short in one sector are not stacked; the unlisted 'Other' bucket is not a sector; a position whose stop is at or past breakeven risks 0 and adds nothing.** **Across both services:** each service publishes its open risk split by sector and side in a NEW table `stockky_shared_heat_sectors` (model `SharedHeatSectors`, created by `create_all()`, no ALTER; one row per service with a small JSON of `<sector>:<side>` to rupees, same fail-open and stale rules as `stockky_shared_heat`), so a bank held in one service counts against a bank entry in the other. Position Stocks: `capital/heat_gate.py` takes the symbol (both callers, long entry and short entry, pass it) and a refusal shows `sector=` and `sector_extra=` in the `HEAT_LIMIT` reason. Real Trade: `risk_engine/engine.py` check 6 adds the surcharge, `AccountState` gets two sector maps (default empty = no surcharge), filled in the three places that already fill the heat fields (`entry_engine/entry.py`, `manual_engine.py`, `main.py`); the reject message names the surcharge. **Switch:** `SECTOR_HEAT_ENABLED` (both control catalogs; off = no surcharge) and `SECTOR_HEAT_MULT` (default 1.5); `ENV_REFERENCE.txt` regenerated. Fail-open: any error means no surcharge. **Checked here:** 8 new Real Trade tests and 6 new Position Stocks tests (surcharge maths, keys and merge, sector split skipping 'Other' and riskless positions, a second bank refused where another sector fits, peer sector counted, short not stacked on long, switch off / unlisted / DB error open); the existing round 50 to 54 heat and ladder tests still pass; catalog and shared-copies checks clean. **Not run here:** the real database path (`publish_sectors` / `get_peer_sectors` against Oracle, and `own_sector_risk` against `ScalpPosition`), because SQLAlchemy is not installable in this workspace. On the VM, after the first entry in a sector, `stockky_shared_heat_sectors` should have a row per service for today.
- **E276 round 59 (1 Nov 2026): the risk ladder in Real Trade - a setup's own closed real trades raise (or, with the brake, lower) its total open-risk limit (real-trade-service).** New `entry_engine/risk_ladder.py` (identical copy of position-stocks `orders/risk_ladder.py`, added to IDENTICAL in `check_shared_copies.py`), `entry_engine/ladder_stats.py` (copy of `setup_stats.py`, only the `setup_stamp` import changed) and `entry_engine/risk_stage.py`. **Linking a closed position to its setup:** Real Trade keeps the setup stamp on the entry decision (flow_json), not on the position, so `portfolio/portfolio.py` now appends " [decision=<id>]" to the OPENED event text of a new position (DEMO and REAL paths; no column, no migration); `risk_stage` reads closed REAL positions (not broker-imported), takes quantity and decision id from that event, the stamp from the decision, and R = net P&L / (initial stop distance x quantity). **Only positions opened after this round count**; older ones, imports and unstamped decisions are skipped. Stage 0 = `DEFAULT_MAX_PORTFOLIO_RISK_PCT` (5 %); stage 1 = 100+ closed trades and a positive 90 % lower bound of average R (6 %); stage 2 = 300+ and a bound above +0.05R (7 %); the last 20 closed trades net negative drops one stage; stage 3, the per-trade risk rise and the daily-loss and per-slot parts are NOT in Real Trade (its size stays with `size_ladder`). `entry_engine/entry.py` sets `account_state.max_portfolio_risk_pct` from the setup's stage just before the risk engine runs; any error or an unknown setup keeps the base. **Switch:** `RISK_LADDER_ENABLED` (real-trade control catalog; off = base limit), `RISK_LADDER_HEAT_STEP_PCT`, `RISK_LADDER_CACHE_S` in `config.py`; `ENV_REFERENCE.txt` regenerated. **Checked:** 3 new tests (tag and parse, closed-position rows into cells, stage and heat limit with the brake, switch and a DB error) and the 10 round 50 heat tests pass; position-stocks ladder tests still pass; catalog, shared-copies and env-reference checks clean. Not run here: the real DB reads (`_load` against TradePosition / TradePositionEvent / TradeDecision) and `portfolio.py` under a real database. With few closed trades and holds of 2 to 5 days, Real Trade will sit at stage 0 for months; that is expected. **Still open in E276:** the heat decision (raise the 5 % base or accept smaller per-trade risk).
- **E276 round 58 (1 Nov 2026): the ladder is also read per time slot - a slot that loses holds the setup at stage 0 (position-stocks).** `orders/risk_ladder.slot_blocked(cell)`: true when the setup's own cell for a slot has 30+ closed trades (`SLOT_MIN_TRADES`) and an average R below 0. `orders/risk_stage.stage_for` reads the current IST slot (`setup_stamp.time_slot`: 09:20 / 09:30 / 10:00 / later), and when the setup's overall stage is above 0 and its cell for that slot is blocked it answers 0, so that entry gets the base heat limit, base per-trade risk and base short cap. It only ever LOWERS the stage; a slot with fewer than 30 trades or no average never blocks (the plan's literal "a stage per slot" would need 100+ trades in every slot and would almost never leave stage 0, so the overall stage stays the base and the slot is the veto). Any error keeps the overall stage. **Switch:** `RISK_LADDER_SLOT_ENABLED` (control catalog; off = overall stage in every slot); `ENV_REFERENCE.txt` regenerated. No signature changed: entry, short and heat paths are untouched. **Checked:** new test (blocked rule, slot 0930 blocked, slot 1000 allowed, switch off) and the earlier ladder / heat tests pass (16 in all); catalog, shared-copies and env-reference checks clean. Not run here: the real DB read and ledger tests (sqlalchemy cannot be installed). **Real Trade ladder NOT built:** Real Trade has no setup_stats, and its stamp lives on the decision row (flow_json), not on the closed `TradePosition`, so a ladder there needs a decision-to-position join and a stats twin first. **Still open in E276:** that Real Trade work, and the heat decision.

Heat limit 5 / 6 / 7 / 8 %; the live short cap stays 5; per-trade risk stays at the stage 2 multiple (x1.5 = 3 %), which is the plan's 3 % ceiling. **Half-Kelly is NOT computed:** with a 3 % ceiling and a stage 2 lower bound above +0.05R, the half-Kelly figure from the lower bound (about 50 x bound in % of capital, taking one unit of risk as the standard deviation of R) is not below 3 % for a setup that qualifies, so it would never lower the size; if you want it as an explicit calculation, say so. No new setting; `RISK_LADDER_ENABLED` turns the whole ladder off. **Checked:** new stage 3 test (sustained, not sustained, needs stage 2 now, brakes, 8 % arithmetic) and the earlier ladder / heat tests pass (15 in all). Not run here: the real DB read (the `older` cells) and ledger tests (sqlalchemy cannot be installed). **Still open in E276:** real-trade-service ladder, per-time-slot stage, the heat decision.

Same switches as round 51 (`HEAT_GATE_ENABLED`, `HEAT_LIMIT_PCT`); fail-open as before; no new variable. **Checked:** 1 new test (6 in the file) passes with the DB stubbed; compile clean.
