- **E276 round 50 / 51 (1 Nov 2026): the total open-risk limit is now shared by both services, with a gap allowance on carried positions (real-trade-service, position-stocks).** **Why:** the two services trade ONE Dhan account, but real-trade-service's 5 % open-risk cap only counted its own stops against its own pool, and position-stocks had no heat check at all, so both could be at the limit together. (The round 50 / 51 work from the pasted session log was not in the round 49 zip, so it is rebuilt here.) **Round 50 (publish + Real Trade check):** new pure `portfolio_heat.py` (identical copy in `risk_engine/` and `capital/`, added to `IDENTICAL` in `scripts/check_shared_copies.py`): stop risk (a stop at or past breakeven counts 0), gap allowance (value x gap %), 95th-percentile overnight gap helper, and one `check()`; new table `stockky_shared_heat` (`models.SharedHeat`, same shape in both services, created by `create_all`, no ALTER) with `execution/shared_heat.py` / `capital/shared_heat.py` (publish own, read peer; a peer row that is missing, from an earlier IST day or older than `HEAT_PEER_MAX_AGE_S` reads as none, so it can never block). Real Trade: `execution/heat_state.py` computes its own risk and gap allowance (swing holds and positions opened on an earlier IST day are carried; allowance = `HEAT_GAP_DEFAULT_PCT`, 1.5 %, of value) and publishes with the equity sync; `AccountState` gets three fields (default 0.0 = the old cap) set at all three construction sites (`entry_engine/entry.py`, `manual_engine.py`, `main.py` dry run); check 6 in `risk_engine/engine.py` now compares own risk + gap allowance + position-stocks risk + the new order with the limit % of the COMBINED pool (own pool alone when the peer is missing); the reject message names each part. Position Stocks: `ledger.own_heat` / `publish_heat` publish its open risk (positions converted to carry-over get the allowance) on the same heartbeat as the exposure. **Round 51 (Position Stocks enforces it):** new `capital/heat_gate.py`; `orders/entry.py` refuses a long entry that would push the shared heat past `HEAT_LIMIT_PCT` (5, same as Real Trade) with a `HEAT_LIMIT:...` candidate-log reason, after sizing and before the order-budget step (the forced 1-share first live order is a probe and is not gated). **Switches:** `PORTFOLIO_HEAT_SHARED_ENABLED` (both catalogs), `HEAT_GATE_ENABLED` and `HEAT_LIMIT_PCT` (position-stocks catalog); `HEAT_GAP_DEFAULT_PCT`, `HEAT_PEER_MAX_AGE_S` in both `config.py`; `ENV_REFERENCE.txt` regenerated. **Fail-open everywhere:** switch off, limit 0, any DB error, or a stale peer = the old single-service behaviour. **Not in this round:** the per-stock 95th-percentile gap is available as `p95_gap_pct` but not yet wired (a flat 1.5 % is used); short entries in position-stocks are not heat-gated yet (their risk is counted once open); the stage-0 heat decision stays open: at 5 % and 2 % risk per trade two full-size trades fill the limit, five fit only at about 1 % each. **Checked:** 9 Real Trade tests (including the real `risk_engine.evaluate` boundary with and without the peer and the gap allowance) and 5 Position Stocks tests pass with the DB stubbed; catalog, shared-copies, table-name and env-reference checks are clean. Not run here (no pytest plugins, sqlalchemy or database in the sandbox): the full suites and the real DB publish / read - run both on the VM.
- **E274 round 49 (31 Oct 2026): the negative-event and momentum-health exits now reach swing positions, and a positive event can add one session to a swing position's last session (real-trade-service).** **Found:** the exit loop's swing branch (1b) ends in `continue`, so the round 45 event exit (3c) and the round 48 momentum exit (3d) sat after it and never ran for a swing position. **Fix:** both blocks moved ahead of the swing branch (1a, 1a2); the event exit now also runs for non-swing positions before the target step, the momentum exit stays swing-only. **New:** `swing/live.maybe_extend` - on the last session, a position in profit with a fresh typed positive event of strength 0.60+ is carried one more session, once; state is one `SWING_EVENT_EXTEND` row in `TradePositionEvent` (no new column), read back by `extra_sessions` so the 15:00 hold check and the overdue backstop both use the longer limit. Stop, trend check and carry vetoes still apply. **Switches:** `SWING_EVENT_EXTEND_ENABLED` (control catalog), `_MIN_STRENGTH`, `_SESSIONS`, `_MAX` in `config.py`; `ENV_REFERENCE.txt` regenerated. **Checked:** 3 new tests pass with the DB stubbed (once only, not on a non-last session, loss, weak or no event, switch off); catalog and shared-copies checks clean. The reordered exit loop was compiled but not run (no pytest or database here).
- **E274 round 43 (31 Oct 2026): a reachable target for shorts (position-stocks-service).** **Found:** a short got the long-style adaptive target, which replayed shorts reached only 3.3 % of the time (33 % needed to break even); down-moves are faster and shorter. **Fixed:** new `orders/short_target.py`, called in `plan_short` after the stop is tightened and BEFORE the geometry / reward:risk floor, the Time Machine and the cost gate, so those judge the NEW target. Two rules, each only ever nearer, never touching the stop, the size or the stop-based budget: (1) an `S4_CATALYST_DOWN` short whose setup cell (slot, else ALL) has 30+ closed trades and 10+ winners takes the cell's typical favourable move from `setup_stats.exit_numbers` (shrunk n / (n + 30) toward the plan target); a legacy-shortlist short is never retargeted from a cell because that cell also holds long entries; (2) one tick above the next HVN wall below (`hvn_below` of the RELIABLE order-flow tape), as `tape_exit.py` already does for open trades, and no new market-data call when the order-flow gate is off. A trimmed target must still pay `SHORT_TARGET_MIN_PCT` (0.5 %). A target that is reachable but fails the 2:1 floor is a refusal; the floor is not loosened. The note is stamped in `flow_json["short_target"]` (record only). The time stop already comes from the setup's own time-to-peak (round 38). Switches `SHORT_TARGET_LEARNED_ENABLED`, `SHORT_TARGET_HVN_ENABLED`, `SHORT_TARGET_MIN_PCT` in the Calibration tab under Entry guards. Tests: `tests/test_e274_short_target.py` (25).
- **E274 round 42 (31 Oct 2026): the Market Board becomes a confirmation for negative-news shorts, not a wall (position-stocks-service, real-trade-service shared copy).** **Found:** the short cycle only looked at names the Board already ranks bearish, and `board_gate_short` refused any short the Board had not caught up with, so a fresh negative event (S4) could not be taken until the Board agreed, which cancels most of the 09:21 window added in round 41. **Changed:** (1) `setup_engine` S4 gets a 4th confirmation `board_bearish` (the Board ranks it bearish with at least `SHORT_MIN_BOARD_CONVICTION`); 2 of the 4 are needed. The fact is only present on the short path, so long setups and Real Trade (which runs the same file from `entry_engine/`, kept identical) are unchanged. `setup_short` builds it (`board_bearish()`). (2) `short_entry.plan_short`: when the news check found a NEGATIVE typed event, the Board gate is left out of the gate walk (`evaluate(skip_board=True)`) and S4 decides; a short that then matches no S4 setup is run through `board_gate_short` and refused as before, so the old shortlist path keeps the Board as a hard rule. Every other fail-closed gate (tick, range, gap, VWAP after 09:45, circuit, depth, structure, quality, order flow, levels, cost) is untouched. (3) the cycle also sends scan names the Board does not list to the news check (Board names first, the pool size unchanged) and keeps an off-Board name only when its quality answer carries a negative event. Switch: `SETUP_S4_BOARD_CONFIRM_ENABLED` (Calibration tab, Entry guards); OFF = the Board is a hard rule for every short and S4 keeps its 3 confirmations. **Behaviour change to watch:** a short on a negative event can now be taken with no Board row at all, if two of tape selling / below VWAP / sector bottom third / Board bearish agree; expect more `S4_CATALYST_DOWN` stamps and a few more news checks per cycle. **Not built:** S2 / S3 short facts, no-setup-no-trade for shorts, short targets from `setup_stats`, event-backed Real Trade entries. **Tests:** `tests/test_e274_board_confirm.py` (17). Full position-stocks suite run here with real pytest on the round 41 and round 42 trees: the same 391 failures and 10 errors (no Oracle or broker in this sandbox), no new failure; the real-trade-service suite gives the same result before and after.
- **E274 round 41 (31 Oct 2026): Position Stocks shorts on negative news may start at 09:21, and a squeeze guard stops the other shorts (position-stocks-service).** **Found:** the short window opened at `SHORT_ENTRY_START_IST` 09:45, so the best minutes for bad news (09:20 to 09:45) were closed, and nothing refused a short into a stock that is being bought. **Changed:** (1) `short_gates.early_open()`: from `SHORT_EARLY_S4_START_IST` (09:21, the first closed bar after the 09:15-09:20 range) to `SHORT_ENTRY_START_IST` only an `S4_CATALYST_DOWN` short may go on. In that window `evaluate(early=True)` skips the too-early refusal and the day-VWAP gate (it rests on too few trades); every other short gate stays fail-closed (price, range, gap, circuit, depth, board, structure, quality, the 13:30 cut-off). `short_entry.plan_short` now asks `setup_short.decide` right after the tape check (before levels and sizing, same inputs as round 40, quote reused from the facts) and refuses a non-S4 short before 09:45 as `SHORT_TOO_EARLY`; an S4 match is sized by the engine (quarter size in the 09:20 slot, half later, slot-ladder promoted / demoted) and the cycle's time check uses the same window. (2) New `orders/squeeze_guard.py`: a short that is not an S4 match is refused as `SHORT_SQUEEZE` while price is at or above the opening-range high (from the recorded bars), the last 3 completed 1-minute bars average more volume than the opening range and than the 3 bars before them, and the tape verdict shows buyers. A missing bar, range or tape never blocks; S4 is never blocked. Switches (Calibration tab, Entry guards): `SHORT_EARLY_S4_ENABLED`, `SHORT_SQUEEZE_GUARD_ENABLED`, `SHORT_EARLY_S4_START_IST`. **Behaviour change to watch:** a short on fresh negative news can now open from 09:21 at quarter size, and a legacy shortlist short into a rising-volume squeeze above the range high is now refused. The shortlist still only lists names the Market Board ranks bearish, so an early S4 short needs the Board to agree until the board-gate round. **Not built:** the Board as one of 3 confirmations for S4, S2 / S3 short facts, no-setup-no-trade for shorts, short targets from `setup_stats`, event-backed Real Trade entries. **Tests:** `tests/test_e274_early_short_and_squeeze.py` (13) and `tests/test_e274_early_short_plan.py` (7); the full position-stocks suite was run here with real pytest on the round 40 tree and on this one: the same 391 failures and 10 errors in both (this sandbox has no Oracle or broker connection), no new failure, 20 more passes.
- **E274 round 40 (31 Oct 2026): S4_CATALYST_DOWN, the short on a negative typed event, now fires on Position Stocks shorts (position-stocks-service).** **Found:** `setup_engine` already had S4, but nothing fed it a short, so every short was stamped as the legacy shortlist. **Changed:** new `orders/setup_short.py` builds the short facts (the negative event on the quality answer, the SHORT opening-range record from `orb_shadow.evaluate(..., side="SHORT")`, the cached quote and VWAP, the pre-open gap, the tape verdict the plan already fetched, the sector third and the priced-in ratio with sign -1) and asks the engine. S4 = negative event, price at or below the pre-open price, a completed 1-minute bar closed below the opening-range low, 2 of 3 confirmations (tape selling, below VWAP, sector in the bottom third); refused when the fall is already priced in. `short_entry.plan_short` calls it after the budget is worked out: a match scales the budget by the engine's size (half size from 09:30, quarter in the 09:20 slot, slot-ladder promoted / demoted) and `short_live` stamps the trade `S4_CATALYST_DOWN` (side SHORT) in `flow_json["setup"]`; a short that matches nothing is stamped `LEGACY_SHORTLIST` (side SHORT) at its normal size. It never refuses a short. Shadow rows are sized the same way but carry no stamp (no `flow_json` column). Switch: `SETUP_S4_ENABLED` (Calibration tab, Entry guards; also off with `SETUP_ENGINE_ENABLED`). **Behaviour change to watch:** a short on a fresh negative event, below the range low, with two confirmations is now taken at half size instead of full size, and `setup_stats` starts filling an S4 cell, so the slot ladder can promote or demote it after 30 closed trades. **Not built:** 09:20 short entries (the short window still opens at `SHORT_ENTRY_START_IST` 09:45), the squeeze guard, S2 / S3 short facts, no-setup-no-trade for shorts, event-backed Real Trade entries. **Tests:** `tests/test_e274_s4_short_setup.py` (13). Not run here: the real pytest suite (no pytest or SQLAlchemy in the sandbox); run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh position-stocks-service tests/test_e274_s4_short_setup.py tests/test_short_step5_shadow_entry.py tests/test_short_live.py tests/test_e269_short_tape_gate_wiring.py`.**
- **E275 round 39 (31 Oct 2026): a learned exit number may change by at most 25 % a week (position-stocks-service).** **Changed:** new `orders/learned_limit.py` keeps one anchor per learned value (`<setup id>|<slot>|<time_stop or momentum>|ttp_min` and `|typical_gain_pct`) and holds the value in use within the anchor +- 25 %. The first time a time-to-peak is seen the anchor is its old fixed minutes (20 for the time stop, `MOMENTUM_NO_NEW_BEST_MIN` for the momentum exit); a typical gain has no old constant, so its first learned value is its own anchor. After 7 days the anchor moves to the value in use, so a real change in a setup is followed at up to 25 % a week. Applied after the round 37 shrinkage in `exit_adaptive.learned_time_stop` and `momentum_hook.learned_numbers`; a limited value adds ` capped 25%/wk` to its source text. Anchors are kept in `<BAR_RECORD_DIR>/_learned_limits.json` (`LEARNED_LIMIT_FILE`, written atomically at most every 30 s); a missing or unreadable file or a failed write starts from the fixed numbers and never stops an exit. Hard ceilings (`EXIT_HARD_MAX_MINUTES` and the 10 / 40, 15 / 60, 8 / 60 bounds) are untouched. Switch: `LEARNED_WEEKLY_LIMIT_ENABLED` (Calibration tab, Exits); `LEARNED_WEEKLY_LIMIT_PCT` (default 25, bounded 1 to 100) is env only. **Behaviour change to watch:** none until cells have 30 trades and peak times; after that a learned time-to-peak can sit at most 25 % away from 20 minutes in the first week (15 to 25), so a very fast setup reaches its learned time stops over several weeks, not at once. **Not wired:** real-trade-service has no learned exit numbers yet. **Tests:** `tests/test_e275_learned_weekly_limit.py` (14), conftest turns the limit off for older tests. Not run here: the real pytest suite (no pytest or SQLAlchemy in the sandbox); run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh position-stocks-service tests/test_e275_learned_weekly_limit.py tests/test_e275_learned_time_stop.py tests/test_e275_learned_exit_numbers.py tests/test_e275_momentum_hook.py`.**
- **E275 round 38 (31 Oct 2026): the flat 20 / 30 / 90 minute time stops of the stagnation exit are replaced by the setup's own median time-to-peak plus a margin once it is learned (position-stocks-service).** **Changed:** new `exit_adaptive.learned_time_stop(db, pos)` reads the trade's `<setup id>|<slot>` cell (round 37 numbers) and returns the minutes `run_stagnation_exit` uses: no-follow-through = the cell's shrunk time-to-peak plus max(5 min, a quarter of it), bounded 10 to 40; flat band = 1.5 x that, bounded 15 to 60; hard maximum = twice the flat band, bounded 30 to the configured `EXIT_HARD_MAX_MINUTES`, which stays a ceiling and never learns. `stagnation_decision` takes an optional `hard_max_min`. A cell under 30 closed trades or 10 recorded peak times, no setup stamp, a read error or `TIME_STOP_LEARNED_ENABLED` off = the fixed `NO_FOLLOWTHROUGH_EXIT_MINUTES`, `STAGNATION_EXIT_MINUTES`, `EXIT_HARD_MAX_MINUTES` exactly as before. A close made on learned minutes says so in the exit reason. Everything else in the stagnation exit (the gain and flat band from the trade's own target, the fading check, slot pressure, the DB switch and `NO_FOLLOWTHROUGH_EXIT_ENABLED`) is unchanged. Switch: `TIME_STOP_LEARNED_ENABLED` (Calibration tab, Exits; env variables 1688 to 1689). **Behaviour change to watch:** none until cells have peak times (several sessions after round 37 is deployed); after that a fast setup (peak in about 5 minutes) is judged flat from about 16 minutes instead of 30, and a slow one later, up to 60. **Not built:** the plan's 25 %-a-week limit on how far a learned value may move (needs a stored previous value; today a value can move as fast as the 300 s cache refreshes it, bounded only by the 10..60 minute limits and the n / (n + 30) shrinkage), the adaptive trail, the 80th-percentile target, the half-sell, the Real Trade side. **Tests that ran:** new `tests/test_e275_learned_time_stop.py` (6: fixed numbers for no cell / thin cell / too few peak times / no stamp / switch off, learned minutes at two shrinkage levels and the slow-setup bounds, the configured hard maximum as a ceiling and the 30 minute floor, a failing read, `stagnation_decision` with and without the learned maximum, source wiring of the exit); removing the ceiling made a test fail; 47 tests across the E263, E275 and catalog files pass with `--noconftest`; `check_control_catalog`, `gen_env_reference` pass; every changed file compiles. **Did not run:** the DB-backed stagnation-exit suites (sqlalchemy cannot be installed in the build sandbox; the exit loop itself was checked by reading and a source test, not executed): run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh position-stocks-service` on the VM. **VM check:** nothing changes on day one (reason texts carry no 'time stop learned'); later, a STAGNATION_EXIT reason with `time stop learned ttp=..` shows a cell is in use.
- **E275 round 37 (31 Oct 2026): signal 2 (no new best price) and signal 4 (typical maximum gain) of the momentum exit now come from the trade's own setup cell instead of fixed numbers (position-stocks-service).** **Found:** round 36 shipped signal 2 at a fixed 20 minutes and signal 4 as wall-only because `setup_stats` held neither the time-to-peak nor the typical gain, and the time a trade reached its peak was never stored at all (`excursion.py` kept only the price). **Changed:** (1) `orders/excursion.py` now records WHEN the highest and lowest prices were first seen in `flow_json["peak"]` (`hi_at`, `lo_at`, epoch seconds; first tick at that price, the entry time when the entry fill itself is the extreme); it starts filling from this release, older trades have none. (2) `orders/setup_stats.py` adds per cell `typical_gain_pct` (median best gain of the cell's WINNERS, a long's highest price or a short's lowest, % of entry) and `ttp_min` (median minutes from entry to that best price), each with its sample count (`gain_n`, `ttp_n`) and `None` below 10 samples; peak times under 0 or over 600 minutes are ignored; `exit_numbers()` gives the value actually used: a cell under 30 closed trades or a metric under 10 samples = the old constant, otherwise w x learned + (1 - w) x constant with w = n / (n + 30) (the plan's shrinkage), the constant being 20 minutes for signal 2 and the trade's own target % for signal 4. (3) `orders/slot_ladder.py` gets `rows_from_positions()` (one row builder now used by the ladder and `GET /setup/stats`, which therefore also shows the new fields) and `cells(db)`, a cached read that ignores `SLOT_LADDER_ENABLED` because that switch is about entry size. (4) `orders/momentum_hook.py` looks up the trade's `<setup id>|<slot>` cell and passes the learned minutes (clamped 8 to 60) and typical gain to `momentum_health.decide`; no setup stamp, thin cell, read error or switch off = the fixed 20 minutes and a wall-only signal 4, exactly as in round 36; the numbers used and where they came from are stamped in each live `flow_json["momentum"]` record. Switch: `MOMENTUM_LEARNED_ENABLED` (Calibration tab, Exits; env variables 1687 to 1688). `momentum_health.py` is untouched, so the Real Trade copy stays identical. **Behaviour change to watch:** none until a cell has 30 closed trades AND 10 of its winners carry a peak time, which cannot happen before several sessions after this deploy (the gain half can fill sooner, it needs no peak time); position rows are deleted after TRADE_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS, so a slow cell may take long. **Not built:** the learned time STOP (flat 20/30/90 minute rules in `eod_squareoff.py` still apply), the 80th-percentile target and the adaptive trail, the half-sell, the Real Trade side. **Tests that ran:** new `tests/test_e275_learned_exit_numbers.py` (9: peak time recorded once at the first tick and only the improved side moves, stamp keeps other keys and survives garbage, cell gets winners-only median gain and time-to-peak for a long and a short, too few samples / no peak / absurd peak = None, shrinkage at w 0.5 and 0.9 and below 30 trades, hook clamps and falls back); breaking the shrinkage, the first-tick rule and the clamp each made tests fail; 64 tests across the E273 slot-ladder, S2/S3, catalog and E275 files pass with `--noconftest`; `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies`, `gen_env_reference` pass; every changed file compiles. **Did not run:** `tests/test_excursion.py` and the other DB-backed suites (sqlalchemy cannot be installed in the build sandbox; the excursion change was tested with a fake database): run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh position-stocks-service` on the VM. **VM check:** after a session, a closed trade's `flow_json` has a `peak` object; `GET /setup/stats` shows `gain_n` and `ttp_n` growing per cell.
- **E275 round 36 (31 Oct 2026): the first exit piece of the plan - a momentum-health score per open trade that tightens at 2 signals and closes a profitable trade at 3 (position-stocks-service; the pure module is copied into real-trade-service, not wired there yet).** **Found:** nothing on the exit side sold into strength: trailing.py, exit_adaptive.py and tape_exit.py only ratchet a stop, trim a target or close a flat trade. **Changed:** new pure `orders/momentum_health.py` (byte-identical copy in `real-trade-service/exit_engine/`, added to the IDENTICAL family in `check_shared_copies.py`) scores five signals on the trade's own 1-minute bars, one sign for long and short: exhaustion (new best price while the tape delta is against), no new best price within `MOMENTUM_NO_NEW_BEST_MIN` (20, until the setup's own time-to-peak is learned), climax bar (volume 2.5x the recent mean with a wick of at least half the range on the profit side), at the next HVN wall or the typical maximum gain, lost VWAP or trigger level (needs a trade that was once in profit). Signals 2 to 5 never fire on a trade that never worked. 2 signals tighten the stop to max(0.15 %, 1.5 x the stock's average 1-minute range) behind price and cut the target to one tick before the wall (never loosen, never raise, at least 2 ticks better); `MOMENTUM_BOOK_N` (3) signals with the trade at least `MOMENTUM_BOOK_MIN_PROFIT_PCT` (0.2 %) in profit close it; less profit only tightens. New `orders/momentum_hook.py` runs after the tape hook in the fast-reconcile loop: Super Order legs through `modify_super_order` with `verify_stop_leg`, a plain short moves only its software target (E114), a booked trade closes through `close_position_now(exit_reason="MOMENTUM_EXIT")` (the stagnation-exit path). `MOMENTUM_EXIT` is a new terminal status in `statuses.FLAT_SELL_STATUSES` (so reconcile resolves its fill) and a green status in the Position Stocks tab. Every live move is stamped in `flow_json["momentum"]`. Switch: `MOMENTUM_EXIT_MODE` live | log | off (Calibration tab, Exits), shipped as `log` (decides and writes log lines only, touches nothing) because none of this has run on the VM; flip to live after reading a day of `MOMENTUM_EXIT[log]` lines. **Not built in this round:** the half-sell (`MOMENTUM_SCALEOUT`; needs the Dhan leg-resize probe, a qualifying trade is closed in full and the record says FULL), the setup's own time-to-peak and typical gain (setup_stats does not hold them yet, so signal 2 uses the fixed 20 minutes and signal 4 only the wall), the trigger level for signal 5 (not stored on the position), the adaptive trail and time stop in trailing.py / eod_squareoff.py (the 1-signal result already carries `trail_gap_pct`, nothing reads it), the Real Trade swing-exit wiring. **Tests that ran:** new `tests/test_e275_momentum_health.py` (18: each signal on and off, forming bar ignored, tighten / never loosen, full and half book, short mirror, bad data = hold, wiring and default-mode checks) and `tests/test_e275_momentum_hook.py` (8, fake DB / broker: tighten moves both legs and stamps, book closes in full with the new status, log and off touch nothing, plain short keeps its broker stop, skips overnight / no order id / no bars / no price, rejected close and throttle, one bad row does not stop the pass); a deliberate break of the exit reason and of the profit check each made tests fail; `check_shared_copies`, `check_control_catalog`, `gen_env_reference` (1682 to 1687 variables) and `scripts/test_repo_scripts.py` pass; every changed file compiles. **Did not run:** the DB-backed suites (sqlalchemy is not installable in the build sandbox; the same 8 tape-hook tests fail on the untouched round 35 zip for that reason): run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh position-stocks-service` on the VM, and the frontend type check. **VM check:** with the default `log` mode, `docker logs` shows `MOMENTUM_EXIT[log]: ... would TIGHTEN / BOOK` lines for open trades (`flow_json["momentum"]` is written only in live mode); read a trading day of them before switching to live.
- **E273 round 35 (31 Oct 2026): the plan's slot ladder ("quarter, half, full by the slot's own results; if it loses it shrinks") was dead code and is now live in the Position Stocks long entry (position-stocks-service).** **Found:** `setup_engine._size` has always honoured a `promoted` set of "<setup id>|<slot>" cells, but nothing ever supplied it, and nothing could shrink a losing cell, so every cell stayed at its starting size (quarter at 09:20, half later) forever. **Changed:** new `orders/slot_ladder.py` reads the setup stamps of the closed trades still held (`setup_stats.summarize`, cached `SLOT_LADDER_CACHE_S` 300 s) and returns per cell with 30+ closed trades: PROMOTED when the 90 % lower bound of its average R is above zero (the engine then sizes it at full size, soft penalties still applied on top), DEMOTED when its average R is below zero and not proven positive (size x `SLOT_LADDER_DEMOTE_FACTOR`, default 0.5, never under the engine floor 0.25). Thinner cells and LEGACY_SHORTLIST keep their starting size. `orders/entry.py` passes `promoted` into the engine inputs (`setup_live.INPUT_KEYS` now allows it) and applies the demotion to the engine's size before the stamp is written. `GET /setup/stats` also returns `ladder_enabled`, `promoted` and `demoted`. `setup_engine.py` is untouched (it must stay byte-identical with Real Trade). Switch: the round 24 `SOFT_GATES_MODE` (veto = the old refusal exactly); new knob `PREOPEN_GAP_HARD_MULT`. **Behaviour change to watch:** a stock gapping 6-12 % up with no news, or 3-6 % down, is now bought at a fraction of the size once the other gates pass, instead of waiting until 11:00. **Tests that ran:** new `tests/test_e273_preopen_gap_penalty.py` (6: no reason, gap-up penalty growing, refusal at twice the limit, gap-down limit, veto mode and missing gap, wiring order after the against-event skip); `test_e273_gap_event` (7, wiring text updated), `test_e273_soft_gates` (6) and the other plain E273 tests; `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies`, `gen_env_reference` (1,673 variables), `test_repo_scripts` pass; `entry.py`, `soft_gates.py`, `config.py` compile. **Did not run:** `entry.py` and the DB-backed `test_group330_wiring` (httpx, SQLAlchemy, pytest missing here): run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh real-trade-service` on the VM. **VM check:** a log line "sized xN by soft gates (preopen_gap xN)" on an unexplained gap, and `PREOPEN_GAP_*` WAITs only for gaps at twice the limit.
- **E273 round 24 (31 Oct 2026): in the Real Trade long entry, VWAP-below, spike chase and gain room become SIZE PENALTIES, refused only past a hard ceiling (real-trade-service).** Port of Position Stocks round 9 (the 31 Oct scorecard named spike chase, VWAP-below and day-gain as the worst skipped groups, but the plan's three-layer design says one soft factor must not end a trade). **Changed:** new pure `entry_engine/soft_gates.py`: `vwap(tick)`, `spike(tick)`, `gain_room(tick, target_pct)` each return (refusal or None, multiplier). The old gates (`vwap_gate`, `day_spike_gate`) are untouched and still decide whether the gate speaks; past the limit but short of the ceiling the size is x0.85 falling linearly to x0.40 at the ceiling (same ramp as round 17), past it the old refusal text and tag (`vwap_below`, `spike_chase`, `gain_room`) stand. Ceilings: `VWAP_HARD_UNDER_PCT` 1.5 % under the VWAP, `SPIKE_HARD_MULT` 1.75 x the day-gain limit, `GAIN_ROOM_HARD_FRAC` 0.25 of the room the target needs. Penalties multiply, floor `SOFT_GATE_FLOOR` 0.25. `entry.py` Gate 4d collects them in `_soft_pen`, multiplies `adj_risk_pct` by the combined value (log line "sized xN by soft gates"), passes them to the S1 walk as penalties and stamps them in `flow_json["setup"]["penalties"]`. Switch `SOFT_GATES_MODE` penalty (default) | veto (the old refusals exactly; junk reads as penalty), Calibration page, Entry guards, read at call time; each gate's own switch (`SPIKE_GUARD_MODE`, `GAIN_ROOM_MODE`, `ENTRY_VWAP_GATE_ENABLED`) still decides whether it speaks. **Not changed:** VIX block, pre-open gap, stop-out symbol, late day, calendar, liquidity, circuit, depth, order-flow, structure, Time Machine and the cost gate all still refuse. **Behaviour change to watch:** a stock a little under its VWAP, or up 4-7 % at the top of its range, is now bought at a fraction of the size instead of waiting; with the 5 % total open-risk cap that leaves room for more positions. **Tests that ran:** new `tests/test_e273_soft_gates.py` (6: VWAP none / penalty growing / refusal past the ceiling, spike penalty and refusal, gain-room penalty and refusal, veto mode and junk, combine floor and ramp, wiring and catalog); `test_e252_day_spike_gate` wiring check updated to the new path (6 pass); the other plain E273 tests re-run; `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies`, `gen_env_reference` (1,672 variables), `test_repo_scripts` pass; `entry.py`, `soft_gates.py`, `config.py` compile. **Did not run:** `entry.py` itself and pytest (not installed here): run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh real-trade-service` on the VM. **VM check:** a log line "sized xN by soft gates (...)" for a name slightly under its VWAP, and `vwap_below` / `spike_chase` WAITs only for deep cases.
- **E273 round 23 (31 Oct 2026): Real Trade's minimum entry quantity follows the cost gate, so a mid or high priced name is not skipped `MIN_QTY` on a small trade cap (real-trade-service).** Port of Position Stocks round 6, and the plan's "ENTRY_MIN_QTY stops being a fixed 3 and follows the cost gate" for the second service. Until now `ENTRY_MIN_QTY` (3) was a flat floor in Gate 5.55: with five positions sharing a small pool and the Rs 3,000 trade cap, a share above about Rs 1,000 sized to 1-2 shares was WAITed `min_qty` even when its target easily paid its costs. **Changed:** (1) `cost_model.effective_min_qty(entry_price, target_pct, fixed_floor, min_trade_value, min_edge_to_cost_ratio)`: in `cost` mode the floor is the smallest quantity from 1 up to `ENTRY_MIN_QTY` at which the SAME cost gate the entry runs next (`evaluate_entry_cost_gate`, so the minimum trade value AND the edge-to-cost ratio) passes; when none does, the flat floor, and Gate 5.6 then judges it as before. It is judged as a CNC entry with the DP charge (the dearer product), so a quantity that passes here also passes Gate 5.6 whichever product is chosen. Never above the flat floor; `fixed`, the cost model switched off (`COST_MODEL_ENABLED`), a floor of 0 or 1, or an unusable input = the flat floor. (2) `entry_engine/entry.py` Gate 5.55 reads the per-mode cost knobs (`_resolve_cost_gate_knobs`) and the Kronos-adjusted target exactly as Gate 5.6 does, and compares the approved quantity with the effective floor; the WAIT text shows the floor that applied. (3) Switch `ENTRY_MIN_QTY_MODE` cost (default) | fixed (Calibration page, Loss-review guards, read at call time; junk reads as cost); `GET /status` shows `entry_min_qty_mode`. **Trade-off:** 1-2 share trades cannot take a partial profit or trail (the reason group 472 added the floor); the plan accepts it because setup size, the 5 % total open-risk cap and the cost gate now limit risk. **Not changed:** the cost gate itself, the risk engine's sizing, the swing lane's own sizing, REAL-only scope (DEMO never had the floor). **Tests that ran:** new `tests/test_e273_min_qty_by_cost.py` (6, plain runner: a small trade passes when its edge pays, the floor stays when nothing below it passes or the minimum trade value fails, fixed / off / junk / zero floors, never above the floor, a passing quantity passes the entry gate for CNC with and without DP and for MIS, wiring before Gate 5.6 and the catalog flag); the other plain E273 tests and `test_group472_min_qty` re-run; `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies`, `gen_env_reference` (1,668 variables, ENTRY_MIN_QTY_MODE now in both services), `test_repo_scripts` pass; `entry.py`, `cost_model.py`, `config.py`, `main.py`, `catalog.py` compile. **Did not run:** `entry.py` against a live account and pytest (SQLAlchemy and pytest are not installed here): run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh real-trade-service` on the VM before deploying. **VM check:** a REAL entry of 1-2 shares appears for a stock above about Rs 1,000 instead of a `min_qty` WAIT, and `cost_model` WAITs still appear where the edge does not pay.

(2) New `risk_engine/effective_risk.effective_max_positions(row)`, the same rule as `effective_risk_pct`: a row saved by an admin through PUT /risk-config wins; a system-owned row (`system-seed`, the migration tags) follows the config value read at call time (accepted 1 to 10, anything else falls back to the stored number). (4) Catalog flag `DEFAULT_MAX_CONCURRENT_POSITIONS` (Risk caps, 1 to 10, shipped 5). **Not changed:** the total open-risk cap `DEFAULT_MAX_PORTFOLIO_RISK_PCT` (5 %) and risk rule 6 stay as they are, so the real limit on five positions is that total: at 2 % per full-size trade only about two and a half full-size positions fit, and five fit only when sizes are smaller (S1 quarter / half size, penalties, VIX). A row an admin saved keeps its number (check `GET /risk-config`; `updated_by` shows who owns it). **Tests that ran:** new `tests/test_e273_max_positions.py` (5: default 5 and the 5 % cap, system row follows config while an admin row wins, bad config values fall back, the env value is read at import, every AccountState uses the effective cap and the catalog flag exists); `test_e47_effective_risk` (6); `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies`, `gen_env_reference --check`, `test_repo_scripts` pass; `entry.py`, `manual_engine.py`, `main.py` compile. **Did not run:** the risk engine against a live account (needs SQLAlchemy and the database): run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh real-trade-service` on the VM. **VM check:** `curl -s localhost:<port>/risk-config` shows `max_concurrent_positions` 5 when `updated_by` is system-owned; if it still shows 3 with an admin name, save 5 through PUT /risk-config (disarmed) or leave it.

The only differences are that the stock is no longer retried all morning and the stats can count these by name. **Tests that ran:** `tests/test_e273_gap_event.py` now 7 (adds the against reason and the wiring order); the other plain E273 tests, `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies`, `gen_env_reference --check`, `test_repo_scripts` pass; `entry.py` compiles. **Did not run:** `entry.py` itself (needs SQLAlchemy): run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh real-trade-service` on the VM. **VM check:** a WAIT row whose reasoning starts `GAP_AGAINST_EVENT` on a day with a positive event and a weak open.
- **E273 round 20 (31 Oct 2026): in Real Trade a gap UP explained by a fresh typed positive event is no longer refused for the gap alone; it is bought only if S1 confirms (real-trade-service).** Port of Position Stocks round 11. A gap up was refused twice until the morning settled (`OPENING_GATE:EXTENDED` above +3 % until 10:00, `PREOPEN_GAP_UP` above +6 % until 11:00) whatever the news. **Changed:** (1) new `entry_engine/gap_event.py`: `explains_gap(event)` (positive typed event, strength at least `GAP_EVENT_MIN_STRENGTH` 0.3), `gap_kind`, `large_gap_penalty` (1.0 = half size for a gap at or above `PREOPEN_GAP_UP_BLOCK_PCT`), `unconfirmed_reason`. (2) `opening_gate.reject_reason(..., waive_gap_up=True)` skips only the EXTENDED check; the day-range, previous-day, stop and data checks still apply. (3) `entry.py`: an EXTENDED hold with an explaining event is waived (the other opening checks re-run), the pre-open gap refusal goes through `_preopen_gap_or_waive`, both record the waived reason; after the S1 walk, a waived gap whose setup is not S1_CATALYST_UP is refused `GAP_UP_NOT_CONFIRMED` (WAIT, gate tag `gap_unconfirmed`); a large explained gap passes `gap_large` 1.0 into the setup engine's penalties (half size). It needs `SETUP_ENGINE_ENABLED` and `SETUP_S1_ENABLED` on; with S1 off every waived gap-up ends in GAP_UP_NOT_CONFIRMED, i.e. the same refusal as before. **Not changed:** gaps DOWN (still refused, no GAP_AGAINST_EVENT row yet); no event / unknown polarity / weak event = the old refusals; the 09:21 floor and the other gates. **Tests that ran:** new `tests/test_e273_gap_event.py` (5: strength and polarity, real reason strings, large-gap penalty and the unconfirmed text, the waiver skips only EXTENDED while the very high and a gap down still refuse, wiring order and switch); the other plain E273 tests (gap event 5, no-shadow 4, opening-gate penalty 8, ORB open check 10, S1 10, stamp 4) pass; `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies`, `gen_env_reference --check`, `test_repo_scripts` pass; `entry.py` compiles. **Did not run:** `entry.py` itself (needs SQLAlchemy): the wiring is checked by text and compile; run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh real-trade-service` on the VM. **VM check:** on a day with a positive event and a gap up above +3 %, the log shows either a buy stamped S1 or a WAIT reading `GAP_UP_NOT_CONFIRMED`, not `OPENING_GATE:EXTENDED`.
- **E273 round 19 (31 Oct 2026): Real Trade's first live setup, S1_CATALYST_UP, stamps and sizes the entries it matches (real-trade-service).** The plan's setup walk for Real Trade (Position Stocks has had S1 since round 2). A missing fact counts as not true. (2) New readers: `watchlist_engine/intraday_news.positive_event` (today's typed positive entry inside INTRADAY_NEWS_MAX_AGE_MINUTES; a keyword-scan hit is not an event), `entry_engine/preopen.gap_for`, and `orb_bars.range_trigger` (or_high, or_low, the close of the newest closed bar after the range, VWAP; {} when stale or unreadable). (3) `entry.py`, before sizing: only a candidate with a typed positive event is looked at (one blocking bar fetch in a thread); a match multiplies `adj_risk_pct` by the engine's size (0.25 in the 09:20 slot, 0.5 later, floor 0.25, soft penalties from the opening gate and regime shrink it) and its stamp (S1_CATALYST_UP, trigger, confirmations, slot) replaces the legacy stamp in `flow_json["setup"]`. - **E273 round 18 (31 Oct 2026): every Real Trade entry is stamped with the rule that took it, in `flow_json["setup"]` (real-trade-service).** The plan's "entry reason recorded" for Real Trade (Position Stocks has had it since E272). - **E273 round 17 (31 Oct 2026): in Real Trade's opening gate the day-high position and the weak previous-day close become SIZE PENALTIES, refused only past a hard ceiling (real-trade-service).** The plan's "soft factor = a penalty, not a veto" for Real Trade (Position Stocks got this in rounds 9 to 12). **Changed:** `entry_engine/opening_gate.py` gains `penalty(mode, tick, now)` returning `{"mult", "reasons"}`: a candidate past `OPENING_GATE_MAX_RANGE_POS` (0.88) of its day range, or after a previous-day close under `OPENING_GATE_MIN_PREVDAY_CLOSE_POS` (0.5), is sized x0.85 falling linearly to x0.40 as it nears the hard ceiling; two penalties multiply, floor `OPENING_GATE_PENALTY_FLOOR` (0.25). `reject_reason` now refuses these two only at the hard ceilings `OPENING_GATE_HARD_RANGE_POS` (0.98, buying the very high) and `OPENING_GATE_HARD_PREVDAY_CLOSE_POS` (0.10, a close in the bottom tenth). `entry.py` computes the penalty once the candidate passes the gate and multiplies `adj_risk_pct` with it (logged as "sized xN by the opening gate"). - **E273 round 15 (31 Oct 2026): Real Trade's ORB is LIVE or off (no record-only mode), and the swing entry skips a pick whose first 5 minutes reversed (real-trade-service).** The plan's "ORB_MODE live ... entry confirmation (RT)" and "skip if the first 5 minutes reverse". Until now `entry_engine/orb_shadow.py` only wrote a verdict onto `flow_json["orb"]` and nothing read it. **Changed:** (1) `config.ORB_MODE` is `live` (default) | `off`; `shadow`, blank and junk read as `live`; the record's `mode` says `live`; every "record only" comment in `orb_shadow.py`, `orb_bars.py`, `entry.py` and `config.py` for this feature is gone. (2) New `orb_bars.open_check(bars, now, max_drop_pct, min_bars)` (pure): from the closed 09:15-09:20 1-minute bars it says `ok=False` when the close of the last range bar is more than `SWING_OPEN_MAX_DROP_PCT` (0.15 %) under the open of the first (`first_5m`), or when the newest closed bar after the range is under the range low (`below_range_low`). `ok=None` (no verdict, buy as before) when there are fewer than `SWING_OPEN_MIN_BARS` (3) closed range bars, no or unreadable bars, or the newest bar is older than `ORB_BARS_MAX_LAG_S`. A bar still forming is never used. (3) New `orb_bars.fetch_now(symbol)`: a blocking fetch into the same cache, because the swing entry pass runs once a day and cannot wait for the background refresh. (4) `swing/live.entries`: after the vetoes and before sizing, a pick with `ok=False` is recorded SKIPPED with `OPEN_REVERSAL: first 5 minutes -0.80%` and the loop moves to the backup pick (E273 round 13 plans one extra). A very large gap (at or above `PREOPEN_GAP_UP_BLOCK_PCT`, the fixed outer limit; the stock's own 95th-percentile history is not stored, so the fixed limit stands in) keeps a 1.0 `gap_large` penalty (half size) that the setup engine sees. (2) a gap DOWN past its limit with a positive event is skipped: `GAP_AGAINST_EVENT:...` (the market has priced something the headline does not show). (parts)`, a per-symbol skip (the next shortlisted name is still tried) that still arms the pullback re-arm from the spike / range reason. A pullback re-arm entry skips the range and spike penalties exactly as it skipped those gates. - **E273 round 7 (31 Oct 2026): REGIME_RULES goes live as a SOFT PENALTY - a weak-market candidate that passes the live floors but misses the stricter ones is sized down, not recorded (real-trade-service).** The plan's "REGIME_RULES_MODE (RT): log -> live soft penalty". Until now `entry_engine/regime_rules.py` only wrote a hypothetical verdict (`REGIME_RULES` log line, `/regime-rules/status`) and changed nothing. **New `regime_rules.penalty(verdict)`:** a test counts only when the candidate FAILS the regime floor but PASSES the live floor (`ENTRY_MIN_REWARD_RISK`, `CANDIDATE_MIN_CONVICTION`); a value already under a live floor is left to the old gates and their exemptions (upper circuit etc.). `depth` = the worse shortfall as a fraction of the regime floor; `mult = 1 - (1 - risk_scale) * min(1, depth / REGIME_RULES_FULL_DEPTH)` (default 0.20, so the shipped weak-market table - R:R 2.5, conviction 65, risk_scale 0.5 - gives x0.5 at a 20% miss, e.g. conviction 60 -> x0.81); `skip` when depth >= `REGIME_RULES_SKIP_DEPTH` (default 0.60) or mult <= 0, which the shipped table can never reach (its deepest miss is 20%), so a refusal needs a stricter `REGIME_RULES_TABLE_JSON`. The worse test decides, never a product; mult is never above 1; neutral and strong markets never change a size; junk / zero / negative depth settings read as the defaults. **entry.py:** the verdict is computed after the R:R floor; a skip is a WAIT before sizing; otherwise `adj_risk_pct *= _regime_pen_mult` right after the VIX and calendar multipliers and before the risk cap becomes a quantity; the E266 conviction upsize is held at 1.0 while a penalty applies (`hold_reasons(regime_rules_mult=...)`, "regime-rules size-down"), so the two cannot cancel each other. `record()` now carries `penalty_mult` / `skipped` and `stats()` counts `penalised` / `skipped`. **Mode:** `REGIME_RULES_MODE` is `live` (default) or `off`; `log`, `enforce`, blank and junk all read as live - no record-only mode is left (plan ground rule). Catalog entry, `/regime-rules/status` text, the frontend probe title and `docs/ENV_REFERENCE.txt` (1651 variables) updated. **Tests:** `tests/test_e273_regime_rules_soft_penalty.py` (15), `test_regime_rules_log_only.py` adjusted to the new mode set; 953 real-trade tests selected by regime / entry / catalog / config / conviction / env / guard / control all pass, plus `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies`, `scripts/test_repo_scripts.py`. **Not run:** the full real-trade suite in one process, the frontend `tsc` / `vite build` (one title string changed), and any live session. **VM check:** after a weak-market session `GET /regime-rules/status` shows `penalised` above 0 and `REGIME_RULES` log rows with `penalty_mult` below 1; the matching entries log `sized x0.xx by the regime rules`. Unset `REGIME_RULES_MODE` in the VM .env (or set `live`).
- **E273 round 6 (31 Oct 2026): the minimum entry quantity now follows the cost gate, so a small pool is not dropped on a mid or high priced name (position-stocks).** Until now `ENTRY_MIN_QTY` (3) was a flat floor: with a small pool, a Rs 1,500 share sized to 1-2 shares was skipped `MIN_QTY` even when its target easily paid its costs. New `cost_gate.effective_min_qty(price, target_pct, floor)`: in `cost` mode the floor is the smallest quantity from 1 up to `ENTRY_MIN_QTY` at which the cost gate passes (when none does, the flat floor, and the cost gate then judges it exactly as before); it is never above the flat floor, so it can only let through a smaller trade whose own edge pays for its costs. `fixed` = the old flat floor; it is also the result when the cost gate is switched off (`SCALP_COST_GATE_ENABLED`), when the floor is 0 or 1, or when an input is unusable. The cost gate itself, the capital shortfall check (`INSUFFICIENT_CAPITAL_FOR_MIN_QTY`) and the forced 1-share first live order are unchanged. **Trade-off:** 1-2 share trades cannot scale out or trail (the reason group 479 added the floor); the plan accepts it because setup size is now what limits risk. **Switch (Calibration page):** `ENTRY_MIN_QTY_MODE` cost (default) | fixed; `/status` shows `entry_min_qty_mode`. `tests/conftest.py` pins `fixed` for older tests (`test_group479_guards`, `test_entry`). Tests: `tests/test_e273_min_qty_by_cost.py` (5, plain runner); the other E273 files re-run; `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies` OK, env reference and file manifest regenerated. **Not run here:** pytest (not installed), so `bash scripts/docker_test.sh position-stocks-service` on the VM before deploying. **VM check:** a small-pool entry with 1-2 shares appears instead of a `MIN_QTY:qty=...` skip; `COST_GATE:` skips still appear where the edge does not pay.

- **E273 round 5 (31 Oct 2026): S2_RS_BREAKOUT and S3_EXHAUSTION (long) go live in the Position Stocks entry, S1 gets its sector third and `priced_in`, and the NO_SETUP refusal exists but ships OFF (position-stocks, one additive key in market-data, one fix in the shared setup_engine).** New `orders/setup_inputs.py` MEASURES the facts the engine needs (it decides nothing): `rs_pct` = the board's side percentile / 100 (board rows rank by |move| over the stock's own average range; a bear-side symbol = 0, a symbol not on the board = unknown); `structure_ok` (daily summary usable, trend not bearish, no fresh unfailed bearish BOS); `or_break` (the ORB closed-bar trigger AND price still at or above the range high), `pullback_hold`, `vol_vs_norm` (last closed 1-minute bar against today's earlier bars: the same-minute norm the plan names has no history yet), `candle` (hammer / bullish engulfing, shooting star / bearish engulfing); S3: `z_move` (largest close-to-close move in the last 30 closed bars over stdev of today's 1-minute returns x sqrt(bars it took); needs 20 returns), `kind` (gap_down_reversal at a pre-open gap <= -1 %, below_open_pullback under the day open, else panic_dump), `failure_candle` (hammer / engulfing within 0.2 % of the spike low), `tape_exhaustion` (reliable tape: sell_exhaustion, selling_absorbed or the long imbalance check "for"), `vwap_stretch` (price under VWAP by 0.25 x daily ATR %, 0.5 % without ATR), `climax_volume` (busiest bar around the low >= 2x mean), `wall_close` (support node within 0.3 % under the price), `liquid` (top `BOARD_LIQUID_TOP_N` by turnover). **S1 extras:** `sector_third` (named sectors with >= 3 stocks, by average change today; fewer than 3 sectors = unknown) and `event_priced_in` (move since the previous close / (`SETUP_PRICED_IN_ATR_MULT` 2.0 x daily ATR %); above 1 refuses S1). `orders/setup_live.py`: `facts_long` takes `allowed` and `inputs` (a whitelist of keys, so a fact can never overwrite price or side), `allowed_set`, `no_setup_refusal`. `orders/entry.py`: the engine now runs with whichever of S1 / S2 / S3 are switched on; sources are read one by one (board payload through the cached `board.build(None, None, [])` that short_entry already uses, the cached structure summary, the 1-minute bars, the tape verdict), so a source that is down loses only its own facts and a missing fact is never true. S2 starts 09:30 at half size, S3 at 09:45 at probation size; priority stays S1, S2, S3. Also fixed in the same block: the "matches ..." log line sat under the penalty-only branch and logged nothing useful (or raised into the debug handler); it now logs on a real match. **NO_SETUP refusal** (`NO_SETUP_MODE` off | live, ships `off`): when live, a long that matches no setup is refused `NO_SETUP: ...` before the Time Machine gate and before any capital moves. It does nothing unless S1, S2 and S3 are all switched on and the engine is on, never applies to a pullback re-arm or reclaim entry (they carry their own trigger), and an engine error is not a verdict (the entry goes on). **Why it ships off:** this round's S2 / S3 code has never run against a live session; the plan says it stays off until S2, S3 and S4 can fire, and S4 (the short path) is E274. Turn it on from the Calibration page after a few sessions show S2 / S3 stamps on trades and the Setup scorecard fills. **Shared engine fix** (byte-identical in position-stocks `orders/` and real-trade `entry_engine/`): S2 treated an unknown gap as 0 (`gap_pct` missing passed the "gap not against" test); it now refuses, as the "missing fact is never true" rule says. **market-data `order_flow_tape.verdict`:** two additive keys, `absorption` and `exhaustion` (S3 reads them; nothing else changed). **Not in this round (still E273):** S2 / S3 on the SHORT side (they ride the short path in E274), ORB_MODE / REGIME_RULES_MODE going live, entry quantity by cost gate (`ENTRY_MIN_QTY`), the other soft-factor gates, and the whole Real Trade half (setup engine on its entries, swing lane without shadow, swing entries only after 09:20). **Starting values, not learned:** top decile (0.9), z 2.0, 30-bar window, 1 % gap-down, 0.2 % / 0.3 % / 0.25 ATR / 2x thresholds above are named constants in `setup_inputs.py` and `setup_engine.py`; each setup is meant to learn its own once a cell has 30 trades. **Switches (Calibration page, default on unless noted):** `SETUP_S2_ENABLED`, `SETUP_S3_ENABLED`, `NO_SETUP_MODE` (off), `SETUP_PRICED_IN_ATR_MULT`. Tests: `tests/test_e273_setup_s2_s3.py` (21, plain runner: every fact measure, S2 end to end and every way it refuses, S3 end to end for the three kinds and every missing part, S1 sector third and priced_in refusal, input whitelist, NO_SETUP rules, the unknown-gap engine fix, wiring order in entry.py), market-data `tests/test_e273_tape_verdict_keys.py` (2); the 17 + 8 + 7 older E273 files and the seven E272 position-stocks files re-run and pass; `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies`, `check_merge_conflicts` OK, env reference 1,648 variables, file manifest regenerated. **Not run here:** pytest is not installed in this sandbox, so `bash scripts/docker_test.sh position-stocks-service` (and `market-data-service`) must run on the VM before deploying; the live `attempt_entry` path with the new block was checked by syntax and by source-order tests only. **VM check:** after a session, trades whose Entry reason reads `S2_RS_BREAKOUT` or `S3_EXHAUSTION` (log line `matches S2_RS_BREAKOUT (...) size x0.5`); the Setup scorecard card starts counting them per slot; `curl` the tape for a symbol shows `absorption` / `exhaustion`; candidate log rows for S1 refusals by `priced_in` appear as plain no-match entries (no new reason text). Flip `NO_SETUP_MODE` to `live` only once S2 / S3 stamps are showing.

- **E273 round 4 (31 Oct 2026): STRUCTURE_EXIT_MODE is now `tighten` by default - a bearish daily-structure break raises the stop of a trade in profit (real-trade-service, plus one rule in calibration-runner).** The plan's "live: tighten the stop now; full exit once structure-exit trades show a positive lower bound". `config.py`: unset, empty or unreadable `STRUCTURE_EXIT_MODE` now means `tighten` (was `log`, record only, since group 415); `exit_engine/structure_exit.py` `mode()` falls back to `tighten` too; the catalog entry ships `tighten` (recommended `tighten`). What it does, unchanged from group 365: only for a position in profit, a fresh bearish CHOCH or a not-failed bearish BOS that formed on a daily bar AFTER the entry day raises the stop to at least breakeven and to LTP - `STRUCTURE_EXIT_TIGHT_ATR` (0.75) x ATR; ratchet only (a stop is never lowered), a losing position is never touched, no or stale structure data = no opinion; it never sells (`exit` mode does, and stays opt-in until structure-exit trades show a positive lower bound). The VM sets no `STRUCTURE_EXIT_MODE` (31 Oct check), so this takes effect on deploy; `STRUCTURE_EXIT_MODE=log` in `.env` brings the old record-only behaviour back. **calibration-runner `autocalib.py` `r_g2_exit`:** it used to step `tighten` back down to `log` whenever G2 failed, which would have undone this on its next apply run (G2 is currently `no_edge` = FAIL). It now only steps the SELLING mode: `exit` -> `tighten` on a G2 fail; `tighten` and `log` are left alone. Updated to match: `tests/test_group415_structure_defaults.py` (default and the junk / empty cases now `tighten`, `.env.example` line), `tests/test_group365_structure_exit.py` (fallback), `calibration-runner-service/tests/test_autocalib.py`, `.env.example`. New `tests/test_e273_structure_exit_tighten_default.py` (5, plain runner: default and junk fallback, a profitable trade's stop rises to at least breakeven and stays under the price, a losing trade or a higher existing stop is untouched, log / off change nothing and tighten never sells, catalog and autocalib agree). **Not in this round (still E273):** ORB_MODE and REGIME_RULES_MODE going live. ORB already feeds S1 through the closed-bar record (round 2) and its only other effect would be the S2 / S4 wiring, so it moves with those setups; REGIME_RULES needs its penalty values designed (it has no live mode today). **Not run here:** pytest is not installed in this sandbox. I ran the existing `test_group365_structure_exit.py` functions through a small shim without their autouse fixtures: 18 pass and the same 7 fail on the ORIGINAL zip as on this one (they need the fixture), so the shim showed no regression, but the real run is on the VM: `bash scripts/docker_test.sh real-trade-service` and `calibration-runner-service`. `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies` OK, env reference 1,644 variables. **VM check:** after deploy the Real Trade `/status` (or the structure-exit stats) shows `mode: tighten`; on a profitable held stock with a fresh bearish daily CHOCH / BOS the exit log shows `STRUCTURE_TIGHTEN` events and `Stop raised to ...`; no sell comes from it.

- **E273 round 3 (31 Oct 2026): BELOW_OPEN stops refusing and becomes a soft penalty that shrinks the size (position-stocks).** The one gate the 31 Oct scorecard showed blocking winners (`OPENING_GATE:BELOW_OPEN`, 68 rows, target first 41.2 % vs stop first 36.8 %, net +0.36 against the entered baseline's -0.11). `screening/opening_gate.py`: new `below_open_mode()` and `below_open_penalty(symbol, ltp)`; the BELOW_OPEN refusal in `reject_reason` now fires only in `veto` mode. The penalty is how far the price is under the day open, in units of the stock's own noise (one unit = `OPENING_BELOW_OPEN_UNIT_ATR_FRAC` 0.1 x its daily ATR %, or 0.3 % when there is no ATR; a stock with 3 % ATR 0.3 % under its open = 1.0, a calmer 1 % ATR stock = 3.0). Price under the PREVIOUS close still refuses (`BELOW_PREV_CLOSE`), and every other opening-gate check is unchanged. `orders/entry.py`: right after the opening gate the penalty goes into a `_penalties` dict; a total at or above `setup_engine.PENALTY_SKIP` (4.0) still skips (`OPENING_GATE:BELOW_OPEN_TOO_FAR`, i.e. about 1.2 % under the open at 3 % ATR); below it the size shrinks to 1 / (1 + total), never under 0.25 (`setup_live.penalty_size`; a matched setup already carries the penalty inside its own `size_mult`, via `facts_long(penalties=...)`), applied with the same lower-of rule as the setup size and the opening-window multiplier, and the penalties are written into the trade's setup stamp (legacy stamp too, so setup_stats can later split penalised from clean entries). **Probation:** it is one gate on a short window, so the size shrink is the safety; the plan's S3 below-open pullback long is a later round. **Switches (Calibration page):** `OPENING_BELOW_OPEN_MODE` (`penalty` default | `veto` = the old refusal), `OPENING_BELOW_OPEN_UNIT_ATR_FRAC`; `OPENING_BELOW_OPEN_FALLBACK_UNIT_PCT` env only. `tests/conftest.py` pins `veto` for older tests (`test_group268_opening_gate.py` asserts the old refusal at lines 104 and 489). **Deliberately not done:** the other soft-factor gates (near day high, spike chase, VWAP distance, pre-open gap, gain room, EMA20 stretch, HVN wall) stay yes/no for now; the scorecard rated them KEEP, so each converts only when its own evidence says so. Tests: `tests/test_e273_below_open_penalty.py` (7, plain runner: unit maths with and without ATR, none when not below open / veto mode / gate inactive, mode normalisation, size floor, a matched S1 carrying the penalty and PENALTY_TOO_HIGH, wiring order); `test_e273_setup_live` (8), `test_e273_setup_engine` (17) and the seven E272 files re-run; `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies` OK, env reference 1,644 variables. **Not run here:** pytest is not installed in this sandbox, so `test_group268_opening_gate.py`, `test_e259_gate_prefilter.py` and the rest of the position-stocks suite did not run - run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh position-stocks-service` on the VM before deploying. **VM check:** in the candidate log `OPENING_GATE:BELOW_OPEN` rows disappear and `BELOW_OPEN_TOO_FAR` appears only for deep drops; an entered trade made under its open shows `below_open` in `flow_json["setup"]["penalties"]` and a `sized down ... (setup x...)` log line; the next scorecard (`--direction long`) shows how those trades did.

- **E273 round 2 (31 Oct 2026): setup_engine is wired into the Position Stocks LONG entry for S1_CATALYST_UP - the first live use of the engine (position-stocks).** New `orders/setup_live.py`: `facts_long` builds the engine's facts from what `attempt_entry` already holds - the typed event polarity on the quality signal (round 10), the ORB record in `flow_json["orb"]` (`range_high`, closed-bar `close_trigger` / `trigger_close`), the cached `/quote` row (`previous_close`, `avg_price`), the stored pre-open gap (new `preopen.gap_of`, the 09:12 final read where there is one; turned into a pre-open price from the previous close) and the live tape verdict (`flow_gate.fetch_verdict`, cached; read directly because `flow_json["tape"]` is stamped later in the entry; only a RELIABLE tape counts: buying at >= 55 % of 10-minute volume with delta not disagreeing, selling at <= 45 %). A missing fact is never true. `classify_long` runs the engine with only S1 allowed. In `orders/entry.py`, right after the ORB / odds records and before the Time Machine gate: a match is stamped as the reason (`flow_json["setup"]` = S1_CATALYST_UP with trigger, confirmations, size) and sizes the trade by the engine (0.25 in the 09:20 slot, 0.5 later; the sizing block takes the LOWER of it and the opening-window multiplier, they do not stack: `extra_factor`). No match = the legacy shortlist stamp and full size, exactly as before. **Deliberately not done:** the `NO_SETUP` refusal ("no setup, no trade") - it would block almost every entry until S2 / S3 (needs relative-strength rank and z-scores), S4 (short path) and the soft-penalty conversion of the gates exist, so it waits for those rounds; sector third (no source wired, so S1 needs tape + VWAP both true today); `priced_in` (not computed yet, so S1 is never refused for it). **Switches (Calibration page, default on):** `SETUP_ENGINE_ENABLED`, `SETUP_S1_ENABLED`; off = every entry is the legacy shortlist at its normal size. **Cost:** none new (one cached tape read, the quote and event the entry already fetches). Tests: `tests/test_e273_setup_live.py` (8, plain runner: match at quarter size in the 09:20 slot and half later, every missing or wrong fact, pre-open price given back, two-confirmation rule incl. unreliable and disagreeing tape, tape sign, lower-not-product sizing, wiring order in entry.py), `test_e273_setup_engine` (17) and all seven E272 position-stocks files re-run; `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies` OK, env reference 1,641 variables. **Not run here:** the live `attempt_entry` path and the older position-stocks pytest files (httpx / sqlalchemy cannot be installed in this sandbox) - run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh position-stocks-service` on the VM before deploying. **VM check:** after a day with a positive typed event on a held-or-candidate name, a trade whose Entry reason column reads `S1_CATALYST_UP` and whose log line `matches S1_CATALYST_UP (...) size x0.25` / `sized down ... (setup x...)`; trades with no match still read `Shortlist (old rule)`; the Setup scorecard card then starts counting S1 per slot (it needs 30 trades per cell before it says anything).

- **E272 round 11 (31 Oct 2026): a fresh negative event now pulls an OPEN trade's stop in (position-stocks).** The held-position half of the plan's E272 live effect, which round 10 left open. New `orders/event_hook.py`, one pass per fast-reconcile cycle in `main.py`, right after the tape hook and before the stop watchdog. For every OPEN Super Order position (not an overnight CNC carry) it reads `GET /events/{symbol}/active` (cached `EVENT_HELD_CACHE_S` 60 s per symbol) and takes the strongest event with a known polarity. When that event is AGAINST the trade (negative on a long, positive on a short), was announced AFTER the trade opened (an event known at entry, or one with no usable announce time, never counts), and has strength >= `EVENT_HELD_MIN_STRENGTH` (0.4), the stop moves so only (1 - `EVENT_HELD_TIGHTEN_FRAC`) (0.5) of its distance behind the live price remains, rounded to the tick. Pure `decide` refuses: no event, unknown polarity, wrong polarity, too weak, known at entry, the same event already used, a stop on the wrong side of the price (the stop watchdog's case), a result no tighter than the current stop, or one that would sit at or past the price. The leg is changed with `modify_super_order(STOP_LOSS_LEG)` and read back with `verify_stop_leg`; an unconfirmed or rejected modify leaves the stored stop alone and retries next cycle. On success `stop_moved_to_breakeven` is set so breakeven.py can never lower it, and `flow_json["event_tighten"]` (last 10: time, event time and type, strength, ltp, old and new stop) is stamped; each event (by announce time) tightens a trade once. A plain short is never touched (E114, its broker stop is fixed by design); feed down = nothing happens. It tightens only and never exits a trade (exits on 3+ momentum signals stay in E275). **Switches (Calibration page, default on):** `EVENT_HELD_TIGHTEN_ENABLED`, `EVENT_HELD_MIN_STRENGTH`, `EVENT_HELD_TIGHTEN_FRAC`, `EVENT_HELD_CACHE_S` (env only). **Cost:** at most one GET per open symbol per minute. Tests: `tests/test_e272_event_hook.py` (11, plain runner: long and short maths, every refusal, once-per-event, a whole pass with stand-ins for the broker and database, unconfirmed and rejected modify, plain short / CNC / feed down untouched, switch off, wiring order); the older E272 position-stocks files re-run (7, 8, 11); `check_control_catalog`, `check_shared_copies` OK, env reference 1,639 variables. **Not run here:** pytest collection of the older position-stocks tests (httpx and sqlalchemy cannot be installed in this sandbox: run `pytest services/position-stocks-service/tests` or `bash scripts/docker_test.sh position-stocks-service` before deploying), a live broker modify. **VM check:** with a held position and a negative event announced after its entry (or `curl /events/<SYM>/active` to see one), the log shows `EVENT_TIGHTEN:` and the position's `flow_json` carries `event_tighten`; confirm the Dhan stop leg matches. **Still open in E272:** the Real Trade 09:20 exit on a negative after-hours event is E274; the `priced_in` field is computed in E273 with the setups.

- **E272 round 10 (31 Oct 2026): typed event fields on the quality check and a LIVE veto - a fresh negative event refuses a long (position-stocks).** The plan's E272 live effect for Position Stocks. `screening/quality_gate.py`: `QualitySignal` gains `event_type`, `event_polarity` (+1 / -1 only), `event_strength`, `event_age_min`, `announced_at`; new `_typed_event_from` (strongest event with a KNOWN polarity from a `GET /events/{symbol}/active` body; polarity 0 and `ok:false` give None) and `_fetch_typed_event` (never raises; feed down = None), called once per quality check. `passes()` refuses a long with `NEG_EVENT: <type> (strength, age)` when the polarity is -1 and the strength is at least `NEG_EVENT_VETO_MIN_STRENGTH` (0.3; strength halves every hour from the announcement, so a 3-hour-old weak event stops vetoing). Positive events never override a veto and never veto; unknown polarity does nothing; the feed being down changes nothing. These fields are also the inputs S1 / S4 of `setup_engine` need (wiring is the next E273 round); `priced_in` is computed at decision time there. **Switches (Calibration page, default on):** `TYPED_EVENT_ENABLED`, `NEG_EVENT_VETO_ENABLED`, `NEG_EVENT_VETO_MIN_STRENGTH`. **Cost:** one more GET to the event feed per quality check (the feed's own cache serves it). `tests/conftest.py` turns `TYPED_EVENT_ENABLED` off for older tests, which fake the httpx client and count its calls. Not done: the tighten-the-stop reaction for an already HELD position (needs the fast-reconcile loop; next round), and the same veto in real-trade (its after-hours veto row from round 6 / 7 already covers its candidates). Tests: `tests/test_e272_typed_event_veto.py` (7, plain runner), the other 5 E272 files and the E273 test re-run, `check_control_catalog` OK, `check_shared_copies` OK, env reference 1,635 variables. Not run here: the older position-stocks pytest files (httpx / sqlalchemy cannot be installed in this sandbox) - run `pytest services/position-stocks-service/tests` in CI or on the VM before deploying. **VM check:** `curl` a symbol with a known negative headline through `/events/<SYM>/active`, then watch `NEG_EVENT:` in the candidate log; and confirm no jump in `EVENT_CHECK_UNAVAILABLE` skips.
- **E273 round 1 (31 Oct 2026): `setup_engine.py` - the S1 / S4 / S2 / S3 classifier (pure module, byte-identical in position-stocks `orders/` and real-trade `entry_engine/`; NOT wired into any entry yet).** `classify(facts)` walks the fixed priority S1_CATALYST_UP, S4_CATALYST_DOWN, S2_RS_BREAKOUT, S3_EXHAUSTION and returns the first setup whose trigger is true and which has at least 2 of its 3 confirmations; none = `NO_SETUP`. Rules as in the plan: a missing fact is never true; unknown event polarity never triggers; `priced_in` above 1 refuses S1 / S4; S1 / S4 start at 09:21 (first closed bar after the range), S2 at 09:30, S3 at 09:45; S2 never fires on a breakout alone (top-decile strength, gap not against, daily structure agrees, closed-bar break or pullback hold); S3 needs a move beyond the stock's own volatility plus a failure candle plus tape exhaustion, long side only in liquid names; Real Trade is long only (S4 is never an entry there); if S1 and S2 fire together S1 wins and S2 is kept as an extra confirmation. Size: 0.25 in the 09:20 slot, 0.5 later (probation), 1.0 only for a cell in `promoted`, halved above the 95th gap percentile, shrunk by soft penalties (floor 0.25), skip `PENALTY_TOO_HIGH` at a total of 4.0. These numbers are starting values (named constants), to be replaced by learned ones. Output carries the `stamp` that `orders/setup_stamp.py` writes. `scripts/check_shared_copies.py` now lists `setup_engine` as IDENTICAL. Tests: `position-stocks-service/tests/test_e273_setup_engine.py` (17, plain runner), `check_shared_copies` OK. **Why no switch yet:** nothing calls it, so a Calibration switch would do nothing; `SETUP_ENGINE` and `SETUP_S1..S4` arrive with the wiring into `orders/entry.py` (next E273 round), together with the soft-penalty conversion of the gates. Not run: the live entry path.
- **E272 round 9 (31 Oct 2026): `scripts/gate_scorecard.py --direction long|short|auto` - short gates can now be read the right way round (script only, no service code).** Until now SHORT_* rows were measured as longs, so a negative number meant the short would have worked. `--direction short` flips every row (positive = the short would have gained; target and stop are on the short's side, and a bar touching both counts as the stop); `--direction auto` flips only rows whose reason starts with `SHORT_`, so one run reads both sides; `long` (default) is the old output. De-duplication also keys on the side. The header line prints the direction. Test: `scripts/test_gate_scorecard_direction.py` (4, plain runner; lifts `side_sign` and `forward` out of the script, since the script itself needs the database). **Not done, on purpose:** the Real Trade setup_stats twin - Real Trade writes no setup stamp until E273, so a twin would have nothing to read; it moves to E273 with the stamp. **VM:** `docker compose exec -T position-stocks-service python - --days 15 --direction auto < scripts/gate_scorecard.py`, compare the SHORT_* rows with the earlier inverted reading before loosening any short gate.
- **E272 round 8 (31 Oct 2026): Real Trade's candidates are now recorded and labelled like Position Stocks' (real-trade-service + position-stocks-service, record only).** Why: the scorecard could only label Position Stocks' skipped candidates; Real Trade's evaluated symbols often had no bars. **real-trade:** new `entry_engine/candidate_outcomes.py` (same pure `label` / `label_rows` as position-stocks, plus `rows_from_decisions` over `trade_decisions` ENTRY rows - ENTER = entered, anything else skipped, long only - and `run_day_rt`); `evaluate_mode` calls `note_candidates` after the candidate fetch, which writes the union of today's evaluated symbols to `<BAR_RECORDS_DIR>/wanted/<day>.json` (`/data/bars/rt/wanted/`; atomic, only when the set grows, capped `RT_BAR_WANTED_MAX` 400, never raises). Real Trade writes only under `/data/bars/rt/`: its outcomes go to `/data/bars/rt/outcomes/<day>.json`.
