- **E272 round 7 (31 Oct 2026): market-hours news reads the typed event feed (veto, trigger, 09:05 pre-open rebuild, new cadence) and the veto row now survives `finalize_nextday_watchlist` (real-trade-service).** **`watchlist_engine/intraday_news.py`:** `run_intraday_news_check` asks `GET {EVENT_URL}/events/active` first (`INTRADAY_EVENT_SOURCE` `feed` default | `legacy`); the old RSS keyword pass runs only when the feed is down, ok:false or unreadable, so an outage changes nothing. Typed entries (type, polarity, strength at announce time, `typed:true`, `bonus` 0) replace the ranking nudge: `intraday_news_bonus` returns 0 for them. Unknown polarity never vetoes or triggers. **Veto (new Gate 5.9 in `entry_engine/entry.py`, `INTRADAY_EVENT_VETO` default on):** `event_veto_reason(db, mode, symbol)` refuses a new long when today's snapshot holds a typed negative event with strength >= `INTRADAY_EVENT_VETO_MIN_STRENGTH` (0.40) announced within `INTRADAY_EVENT_VETO_MAX_AGE_MINUTES` (360, longer than the 3 h news window), or when today's next-day watchlist holds a veto row for that mode and symbol (consumed or not). A typed negative beats a typed positive in the merge; a typed negative lives to the veto window, everything else to the news window. Fail-open on anything unreadable. **Trigger (`INTRADAY_EVENT_TRIGGER` default on):** from 09:20 a typed positive of type results / order / deal (`INTRADAY_EVENT_TRIGGER_TYPES`), strength >= 0.60, announced within 30 min, queues a `TradeCandidate` (`source_tab=intraday_event`, not overnight priority), at most 3 per mode per day (kept in the snapshot, so a restart does not repeat), only with a preview price (the drift gate needs a reference), never for a symbol already queued or held. Every normal gate, hard veto and the risk engine still apply to it. **09:05 rebuild:** once a day between 09:05 and 09:20 the pre-open list (today's `NextDayWatchlistEntry` rows) is rebuilt from the feed: a symbol with no row gets one (positives at or above `AFTERHOURS_SCAN_MIN_INJECT_SCORE`) and new rows are injected through `_inject_nextday_watchlist_candidates`; a negative turns an existing row into a veto row or adds one; it retries each minute while the feed is down and is marked done only after a successful read. **Cadence (`execution/auto_pilot.py`, follows `AFTERHOURS_CADENCE_V2`):** every 60 s from 09:00 to 09:20, every 180 s to 15:30 (window end 15:30; `INTRADAY_V2_*` values), the sleep capped at the 09:20 and 15:30 boundaries; v2 off = the old 15-minute loop to 15:45. `/status` shows the new values. **`afterhours_scan.finalize_nextday_watchlist`:** veto rows are no longer trimmed or counted in the top N; the shortlist (return value) holds tradable names only, vetoes stay unconsumed for the 09:00 pre-pick (which logs and consumes them) and the E274 09:20 exit, and the FINALIZED Telegram message lists them. `event_reader.hits_from_events` now also returns `published`. Control catalog: `INTRADAY_EVENT_SOURCE`, `INTRADAY_EVENT_VETO`, `INTRADAY_EVENT_TRIGGER` (`check_control_catalog.py` ok). `FILE_MANIFEST.txt` regenerated (also picks up the round 3-5 files it had missed). **Tests:** new `tests/test_e272_intraday_event_feed.py` (26 tests: entries, merge, veto reader, trigger rules, whole feed pass, 09:05 rebuild, feed-down fallback, cadence, finalize with veto rows, wiring); it runs without sqlalchemy (`python3 tests/test_e272_intraday_event_feed.py`). `tests/test_intraday_news.py` is pinned to `INTRADAY_EVENT_SOURCE=legacy` and the old cadence (it covers the RSS pass). **Not run here:** the sandbox has no sqlalchemy / httpx and no PyPI access, so the full real-trade-service suite (including `test_intraday_news.py`, `test_afterhours_scan_orchestration.py`, the entry tests), `tsc` and `npm run build` still need the CI run. **Not in this round:** tightening a held position's stop on a negative event (PS side, E272 live effects) and the RT 09:20 exit on a negative after-hours event (E274); Real Trade candidate log and scorecard.**
- **E272 round 6 (31 Oct 2026): the after-hours scan is now a reader over the typed event feed, a negative event is kept as a veto row, and the scan runs on the new cadence (real-trade-service).** First half of the news rework (`intraday_news.py` is the next round). **New `watchlist_engine/event_reader.py`:** reads `GET {EVENT_URL}/events/active`; each typed record (type, polarity, strength) becomes a `NextDayWatchlistEntry` hit. Polarity 0 never becomes a hit; a positive scores strength x 100 with strength taken at the announce time (the feed halves strength every hour, so read-time strength would be about 0 by 08:45); two independent publishers lift strength x1.15, one positive source is capped at 0.70 (Moneycontrol and Moneycontrol-google count as one); a negative becomes a veto row (score 0, full weight even from one source) and beats a positive for the same symbol. No schema change: type, polarity and strength travel in `catalyst_source` as `<source>|ev=<type>|p=<+1|-1>|s=<x>` (`encode_tag` / `decode_tag`, 64 characters at most). **`afterhours_scan.py`:** the feed is asked before the RSS loop; `AFTERHOURS_EVENT_FEED_MODE` `both` (default: typed hits go over the keyword hits, the old RSS scan still runs) | `feed` (RSS only when the feed is down) | `legacy`; a feed that is down, ok:false or unreadable changes nothing. Upsert rule `_upsert_wins`: a veto replaces a non-veto row, a stored veto is never replaced by a positive, otherwise the higher score wins as before. The Telegram summary marks a veto. **`execution/auto_pilot.py`:** the pre-pick never injects a vetoed symbol and puts the typed event into the candidate's `raw_payload`; new cadence behind `AFTERHOURS_CADENCE_V2` (default true): 15:30-20:00 every 30 min, 20:00-08:00 every 2 h, 08:00-09:00 every 10 min (`AFTERHOURS_V2_*` values; the sleep is also capped at 20:00); false = the old cadence exactly. Both switches are in the control catalog (restart needed). `tests/conftest.py` turns both off for older tests. **Not in this round:** the 09:00-09:20 every-minute loop, the 09:05 pre-open rebuild, 3-minute polling in session, `intraday_news.py`, the 09:20 exit on a negative event (E274), the entry-time veto in Real Trade's entry engine (E273/E274); `finalize_nextday_watchlist` still trims to the top N, which can drop a veto row. **Tests that ran:** `tests/test_e272_afterhours_event_reader.py` 19 (tag round trip, hits, merge, type weights equal to the feed's table, cadence table, the sleep function cut out of auto_pilot with a stand-in config, wiring by source text) and `tests/test_e272_afterhours_scan_feed_integration.py` 7 (`run_afterhours_scan` end to end with stand-ins for httpx, models, symbol_master and config and a fake session: both / feed / feed-down / legacy, veto rows, veto stands, Telegram veto line); `check_control_catalog.py`, `gen_env_reference.py --check` (1,614 variables), `check_shared_copies.py`, `check_merge_conflicts.py` pass. **Did not run:** pytest, the real database upsert, `test_afterhours_cadence.py` and the rest of the real-trade suite (sqlalchemy and httpx cannot be installed here), a live session; `docs/FILE_MANIFEST.txt` not regenerated. **Live check after deploy:** the scan log shows `event feed N event(s) -> M typed hit(s), K veto(es)`; `trade_nextday_watchlist.catalyst_source` of a feed-backed row carries `ev=` and `p=`; scan ticks land 30 minutes apart in the evening.

- **E272 round 3 (31 Oct 2026): every candidate gets labelled with what price did next, and the setup stamps can be read (position-stocks).** New `orders/candidate_outcomes.py` (pure labelling + file I/O): for each `scalp_candidate_log` row, traded or skipped, the move at +5, +15, +30 and +60 minutes and the best / worst move inside 60 minutes (`mfe60`, `mae60`), from the recorded 1-minute bars; a short (reason starting `SHORT_`) has its sign flipped; a horizon past the end of the data is `null`, a row with no bars is `no_bars`, never guessed; one row per (symbol, gate, 5-minute bucket). `scripts/candidate_outcomes_job.py --day YYYY-MM-DD` (run inside the container after 15:30) saves `/data/bars/outcomes/<day>.json` and prints rows / labelled / no_bars / deduped. Setup stamp:** new `orders/setup_stamp.py` (pure); `orders/entry.py` stamps `flow_json["setup"]` (id, trigger, confirmations, penalties, size multiplier, time slot 0920 / 0930 / 1000 / later, side) just before the Time Machine gate. Until setup_engine exists (E273) every entry reads `LEGACY_SHORTLIST`, which is the baseline the new setups will be compared with; a stamp already present is kept (first reason wins). No database column. Switch `SETUP_STAMP_ENABLED` (true). **2. setup_stats:** new `orders/setup_stats.py` (pure): per setup and slot, trades, wins, win rate, average R (net P&L / rupees at risk at the stop) and a 90 % lower bound (standard error across session days when 5 or more days exist, else across trades); a cell under 30 trades reports `NOT_ENOUGH_DATA` and no lower bound, so it can never promote a setup. Not yet read by any route or by the ladder (E276). **3. Final pre-open read:** `screening/preopen.py` `due_final()` / `record_final()` / `run_final()`; `main.py` runs it from `PREOPEN_FINAL_TIME_IST` (09:12) until the bell, once a day; a final gap replaces the 09:10 one, a symbol with no fresh final price keeps its 09:10 gap. Switch `PREOPEN_FINAL_READ_ENABLED` (true). The percentile-of-own-gaps rule is E273/E274, not here. **4. Results-day split:** `screening/quality_gate.py` `earnings_split_reason()`: for a stock whose results are dated today, the typed event feed (`GET /events/{symbol}/active`, type `results`) gives the publication time; before `EARNINGS_KNOWN_BEFORE_IST` (09:00) = known event, not blocked; later in the session = blocked until `EARNINGS_REACTION_WAIT_MIN` (5) minutes after publication; no time found, the feed down, or a time from another day = blocked exactly as before. Switch `EARNINGS_SPLIT_ENABLED` (true; false = the old block). The feed is asked only for symbols with results dated today. Every other gate (gap-up refusal, quality floors, negative news) still applies to a stock that is no longer blocked. **Not in this round:** the real-trade `event_risk.py` split, `candidate_outcomes.py` / the recorder on every candidate, the entry-reason column in the frontend, `setup_stats` routes. **Tests that ran** (no pytest / sqlalchemy / httpx here; plain-function runners, `models` stubbed): `tests/test_e272_setup_stamp_stats.py` 8, `tests/test_e272_final_preopen_and_results_split.py` 11; `gen_env_reference.py --check`, `check_control_catalog.py`, `check_shared_copies.py`, `check_merge_conflicts.py` pass. **Did not run:** the full position-stocks suite (existing quality-gate, preopen and entry tests), `tsc`, a live session - run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh position-stocks-service` on the VM. **Live check after deploy:** `GET /status`-style preopen status shows `final_day` = today and `final_updated` above 0 after 09:12; a new trade's `flow_json` holds `setup.id = LEGACY_SHORTLIST`; on a results day with a pre-09:00 headline the candidate log no longer shows `EARNINGS_TODAY` for that stock.
- **E271 (30 Oct 2026): the tape work is now measurable - tape exits are recorded on the trade, both services expose tape stats, market-data reports tape health, a tape report script and a calibration card read them; closes E270 items 4 and 5.** Nothing that places, sizes or exits a trade changed; every addition is record-only or read-only. **Found while building:** `GET /internal/flow-status` carried only counters (rows, intervals, skips) and no `reliable` / coverage, so the E268 live check "reliable: true, coverage above 35 %" could not be read from it. `order_flow_tape.status()` now also returns `symbols_today`, `reliable_symbols`, `reliable` (at least one of today's symbols passes the same rule the gate uses), `avg_coverage_pct` and `unreliable_reasons`; the old keys are unchanged. **Recording:** position-stocks `orders/tape_hook.py` stamps every real tape move on `flow_json["tape_exit"]` (time, ltp, old / new stop and target, reason, plain-short flag; newest last, at most 10; `flow_gate.stamp_tape_exit`, never raises, a failed stamp never undoes the move); real-trade already wrote a `TAPE_TIGHTEN` position event. Both `flow_gate` copies count `exit.applied` (moves really made, not just decided; real-trade bumps it after the commit in step 5b). **Reading:** new admin-only `GET /flow/stats` in both services (gate and exit counters since the last restart, both modes, `FLOW_GATE_MIN_AGAINST`, `FLOW_EXIT_MIN_AGAINST`, and market-data's tape status through one 1.5 s call, null when it does not answer). Position-stocks `GET /trades/history` rows gain `tape` (entry verdict) and `tape_exit`; real-trade `GET /positions/{mode}/history` rows gain `tape` (read from the latest ENTER decision for the symbol made within an hour before the position opened) and `tape_tightens` (count of TAPE_TIGHTEN events), two bounded queries per page, fail-open. **Item 4:** `scripts/flow_tape_report.py` (stdlib, read-only): buckets trades by adverse checks at entry (0, 1, 2, 3+) and compares what the `FLOW_GATE_MIN_AGAINST` cut-off would have refused with what it allowed (KEEP / RELAX / INCONCLUSIVE / NOT_ENOUGH_DATA, 90 % interval with the standard error clustered by session day, at least 30 trades and 5 days per group), checks whether MIN_AGAINST=1 would help, and for exits compares tape-tightened with other trades and gives the loss avoided against the original stop as an UPPER BOUND. `--store FILE` appends and de-duplicates rows because position-stocks deletes closed trades after `TRADE_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS` (3). **Limit that matters:** with `FLOW_GATE_MODE=live` a trade with 2+ adverse checks is refused, so the "blocked" group stays empty and the cut-off cannot be judged; set `FLOW_GATE_MODE=log` for a sample of sessions to collect it (the report says so). **Item 5:** Calibration page, new section "Order-flow tape" (`components/calibration/TapePanel.tsx`, helpers in `tapeView.ts`): reliability and coverage, entry gate refused / no tape / unreliable / errors, exit asked / applied / held, warnings (market-data silent, more than half of entry checks without a tape, call errors, "asked to tighten but nothing applied"). Read-only, polls every 30 s while the tab is visible. **CI that was already red and is now fixed:** `docs/ENV_REFERENCE.txt` regenerated (FLOW_RECORD, FLOW_TAPE_ENABLED from E268 were missing); `frontend/src/components/app/TabButton.tsx` and `BackendStatusDot.tsx` were back in the tree and `appHelpers.test.mjs` requires them gone (nothing imports them), deleted; `calibration.test.mjs` pinned the exact Run-all condition and now includes the new section; `docs/FILE_MANIFEST.txt` rewritten from disk. **Tests that ran** (no pytest / SQLAlchemy / httpx in the build sandbox; plain-function runners, SQLAlchemy / models / dhan_client stubbed): `scripts/test_flow_tape_report.py` 11; position-stocks `tests/test_e271_tape_record_and_stats.py` 8 (hook records and counts a Super Order move and a plain-short target move, log mode and a refused loosening leave no record, a failing stamp keeps the move, `_row_tape`, route wiring) plus E269 2 and E270 12 still pass; real-trade `tests/test_e271_tape_history_and_stats.py` 5 (history join and tighten count with a fake session, fail-open, route and counter wiring) plus E270 7; market-data `tests/test_e271_tape_status_health.py` 3 plus the 24 E268 tape tests (the route test skips without fastapi); the full frontend `npm test` chain, 46 files including the new `tape.test.mjs`; `check_shared_copies.py`, `check_control_catalog.py`, `gen_env_reference.py --check`, `check_merge_conflicts.py`, `check_table_name_collisions.py` pass; `audit_route_auth.py` lists neither new route as open. **Did not run:** the full position-stocks, real-trade and market-data suites, the E268 "gate is wired before ..." tests and `scripts/test_short_live.py` (they import `entry.py` / httpx / SQLAlchemy), the two new `plan_short` cases in `test_short_step5_shadow_entry.py`, the real `evaluate_mode` step 5b, `tape_hook` and the history joins against a real database, `tsc` (no node_modules here), and a live session - run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh position-stocks-service`, `real-trade-service` and `market-data-service` on the VM. **Live check after deploy:** `GET /internal/flow-status` shows `reliable: true` and `avg_coverage_pct` above 35 after about 8 minutes of market time; Calibration -> Order-flow tape shows the same for each service; after the first tape tightening `flow_json` of that trade holds `tape_exit` and `exit.applied` rises. **Not changed on purpose:** `FLOW_GATE_MIN_AGAINST` 2 and `FLOW_EXIT_MIN_AGAINST` 3 stay until the report has data; `FLOW_EXIT_MODE` still ships `live`. Not built: a bar-based counterfactual for tape exits (needs the recorded 1-minute bars). **Added the same day, per-symbol checklist view:** Calibration -> Order-flow tape now also has "One symbol: the six checks" (`TapeSymbolCard.tsx`, helpers in `tapeSymbolView.ts`): type a symbol or tap an open trade and see the six items of the order-flow checklist (profile VAH / VAL / POC, HVN / LVN, footprint, imbalance / absorption / exhaustion, delta, big prints) as for / against / neutral for a long or a short (the short toggle only on Position Stocks), the levels behind them and the tape's coverage; polls every 15 s while visible; read-only. Backed by a new admin-only `GET /flow/symbol/{symbol}` in both services (a plain-symbol check, `[A-Za-z0-9&_-]{1,20}`, else 400; one 1.5 s call to market-data's `GET /flow/{symbol}`; never raises). Tests: `tapeSymbol.test.mjs` (frontend chain now 47 files, all pass), route and symbol-check tests added to both E271 backend test files (9 and 6 pass). Not run here: `tsc`, a live session.
- **E270 (30 Oct 2026): the order-flow tape now also tightens EXITS - a reliable adverse tape pulls an open trade's stop in and trims its target to the next HVN wall, in both services, through one shared pure decision module; includes the item-7 audit of the short exit paths.** Follows E268/E269 (tape on entries). **Audit (read only):** every short exit runs through `orders/short_live.py`, `orders/plain_short.py` (`run_plain_short_guard`), `orders/stop_watchdog.py` and `orders/eod_squareoff.py::run_short_squareoff` (14:55); a plain short's broker stop is never trailed (E114), only its software target can move. **New:** `tape_exit.py` (stdlib only, no I/O, no config; byte-identical in `real-trade-service/exit_engine/` and `position-stocks-service/orders/`, registered in `scripts/check_shared_copies.py`): `decide(...)` returns HOLD or TIGHTEN with `new_stop` / `new_target`. LONG and SHORT are mirrored. Needs a RELIABLE tape, at least `min_against` (3) adverse checks and at least one of them `delta` or `imbalance`. The stop tightens only when the trade is `min_profit_pct` (0.3 %) in profit or the price has used `near_stop_frac` (0.5) of the way from entry to the stop; the new stop sits `gap_pct` (0.25 %) behind the live price, moves by at least two ticks and stays short of the target. The target is only trimmed nearer, to one tick before the next HVN wall, and must still pay `min_target_pct` (0.5 %). A stop is never loosened, a target never raised; unreliable / missing tape, a bad number or an error = HOLD. **Wiring:** `flow_gate.exit_check` (both copies; async in real-trade, sync in position-stocks) makes one uncached call with `FLOW_EXIT_TIMEOUT_S` (0.6 s). `market-data-service/order_flow_tape.py` verdict now carries `price`, `hvn_above`, `hvn_below`. Position-stocks: new `orders/tape_hook.py::run_tape_exit`, called in `main.py` after trailing and before the stop watchdog / plain-short guard, so Super Order longs, Super Order shorts and plain shorts all get it; Super Order legs move through `modify_super_order` (stop read back with `verify_stop_leg`), a plain short only gets `pos.target_price` changed (its broker stop is never touched). Real-trade: step 5b in `exit_engine/exit.py::evaluate_mode` before HOLD, DEMO and REAL, updates the software `current_stop` / `current_target` (this repo has no `modify_super_order` in real-trade; the optional broker safety-net stop follows `current_stop` through `broker_stop.sync_stops`) and writes a `TAPE_TIGHTEN` position event. **Switches** (both services, control catalogs updated; auto-calibrator protected for MODE and MIN_AGAINST): `FLOW_EXIT_MODE` live (default) | log | off; `FLOW_EXIT_MIN_AGAINST` 3; `FLOW_EXIT_MIN_PROFIT_PCT` 0.3; `FLOW_EXIT_NEAR_STOP_FRAC` 0.5; `FLOW_EXIT_STOP_GAP_PCT` 0.25; `FLOW_EXIT_MIN_TARGET_PCT` 0.5; `FLOW_EXIT_MIN_INTERVAL_S` 20; `FLOW_EXIT_TIMEOUT_S` 0.6; `FLOW_EXIT_SAME_DAY_ONLY` 1 (real-trade only). **Tests that ran** (no pytest / SQLAlchemy / httpx in the build sandbox; plain-function runner, SQLAlchemy / models / dhan_client stubbed for the hook cases): `tests/test_e270_tape_exit.py` in both services (pure decision: mirroring, never-loosen, never-raise, HVN trim, required delta/imbalance, unreliable HOLD, bad numbers; position-stocks hook: Super Order both legs, plain short touches only the target, log mode changes nothing, a loosening decision is refused); `check_shared_copies.py` and `check_control_catalog.py` pass. **Did not run:** the real `evaluate_mode` step 5b, `tape_hook` against a real database, and `service-tests.yml` - run on the VM or CI. **Not done:** tape shadow report script (item 4), calibration page card (item 5). **Watch:** `FLOW_EXIT_MODE` ships as `live`; read the `TAPE_EXIT` log lines / `TAPE_TIGHTEN` events for a few sessions before raising `FLOW_EXIT_MIN_AGAINST`, or set `log` first.

- **E267 (30 Oct 2026): log noise from the Yahoo live socket is one short line a minute, and the first AngelOne socket failure is a WARNING, not an ERROR.** 30 Oct 15:00 IST VM log after the E266 deploy: `market-data-service` printed a full `yfinance ... ConnectionClosedError: no close frame received or sent` traceback plus `Attempting to reconnect...` every few seconds (about 45 times in 4 minutes), burying real errors; and `position-stocks WS: error (did not receive a valid HTTP response) - reconnecting in 3.0s` was logged at ERROR although the socket was connected again the same second. **Fix:** `yahoo_ws_feed._YfLiveNoiseFilter` on the `yfinance` logger keeps the first drop of each 60 s as ONE WARNING without a traceback ("N more in the last minute not shown") and hides the rest; only those two yfinance messages are touched, yfinance still reconnects by itself and nothing about the feed changes. `position-stocks-service/feed/ws_client.py`: a socket error is WARNING while the reconnect backoff is still at its start value (the first failure after a good connection) and ERROR on a repeat. - **E266 (30 Oct 2026): trade size now follows an internal conviction score - a sure setup gets up to half of the service's pool, a doubtful one a smaller trade (was: every trade about the same rupees, Rs 14,000 idle).** 30 Oct a Position Stocks trade hit its ~2.5 % target and paid ~Rs 20: the size was fixed by rules (Position Stocks: risk % / 5 slots / stop, slot-capped at 30 % of the pool; Real Trade: Rs 3,000 flat cap and 25 % single-position cap, ~Rs 1,750 of a ~Rs 7,000 share) whatever the setup. **What changed:** each entry gets a 0-100 score and a size multiple. *Position Stocks* (`orders/conviction.py`): board conviction 0.30, reward:risk 0.20 (UPSIDE-ONLY: the 1:2 floor is neutral, a wider adaptive target such as 1:3 raises it), volume pace 0.15, technical score 0.15, zone 0.10, catalyst 0.10; a short uses bearish inputs and is capped at x2. The multiple scales the old budget in `capital/ledger.reserve_capital(conviction_mult=...)` AFTER the slot cap. *Real Trade* (`entry_engine/conviction_size.py`): candidate conviction 0.35, board 0.20, reward:risk 0.15, regime strength vs its gate 0.15, entry freshness (drift vs ATR limit) 0.15; the multiple moves risk_engine's two RUPEE caps (concentration 5c and max trade value 5c-ii) through `OrderIntent.size_mult / size_ceiling_pct`, REAL only. Score <= 35 -> x0.5, 50 -> x1.0 (an entry with nothing known scores exactly 50 = the old size), >= 80 -> x3.0. **Safety:** an unknown input is neutral and can never lift a score (fewer than two known inputs = the old size); an upsize never takes more than 50 % of the service's pool / equity (`CONVICTION_MAX_POOL_PCT` / `CONVICTION_MAX_POSITION_PCT`), never exceeds the plain per-trade risk at the trade's own stop (Position Stocks), is never below the old size, and is HELD at x1.0 on a losing day (`CONVICTION_NO_UPSIZE_ON_LOSS_DAY`), in a regime override, and while a VIX / calendar / opening-window size-down is in force (Real Trade, because those scale the share count before the rupee caps). Real Trade's per-trade RISK cap, cash, 50 % capital-share cap, portfolio risk cap, daily-loss caps and loss-streak brake, and every Position Stocks step after sizing (depth / VIX / calendar / opening size-down, ENTRY_MIN_QTY, cost gate, kill switches, shared order budget) are unchanged. A smaller multiple can leave fewer shares than ENTRY_MIN_QTY, which then waits instead of taking a 1-2 share trade. Targets and stops are not changed: reward:risk stays the adaptive 1:2 floor up to the target cap. Every entry logs `conviction N -> xM size` with the parts, and the Telegram BUY message and the Real Trade PLACED event carry the same line. **Switches** (all live on the Real Money Calibration page, Money safety; protected from the auto-calibrator): `CONVICTION_SIZING_ENABLED` (false = the old fixed size), `CONVICTION_MAX_MULT`, `CONVICTION_SHORT_MAX_MULT` (Position Stocks), `CONVICTION_MIN_MULT`, `CONVICTION_MAX_POOL_PCT` / `CONVICTION_MAX_POSITION_PCT`, `CONVICTION_SCORE_LOW / MID / HIGH`, `CONVICTION_NO_UPSIZE_ON_LOSS_DAY`. The maths file `conviction_sizing.py` has two byte-identical copies (checked by `scripts/check_shared_copies.py`). **Tests:** new `tests/test_e266_conviction_sizing.py` in both services (maths, ledger budget and ceilings, scoring, risk-engine caps with every other limit still binding, a real `attempt_entry` buying more for a sure setup); older tests run with the feature off through an autouse fixture (as for E252); `tests/test_short_live.py`, `tests/test_regime_hooks.py` pins and `scripts/test_short_live.py` fakes updated for the new `conviction_mult` argument. **Not run:** nothing against live Dhan; watch the `conviction` log lines for a few sessions before raising `CONVICTION_MAX_MULT`. A bigger position wins more and loses more by the same factor.
- **E265 (30 Oct 2026): one transient database error no longer refuses a Position Stocks BUY ("symbol lock check could not reach the database (DatabaseError)").** Cause (code): `capital/shared_symbol_lock.py::try_claim` sent ANY exception (a `DatabaseError` from a slow or reconnecting Oracle, a pool timeout) straight to fail-closed (group 448), so a single blip refused the entry and sent the alert, which showed only the class name. The same burst was seen on 29 Oct (E253, slow Oracle, DPY-4024); fail-closed itself is kept on purpose (two services, one Dhan account). **Changes (position-stocks only):** (1) a database-driver error (sqlalchemy `DBAPIError` incl. `DatabaseError` / `OperationalError`, a pool `TimeoutError`, `PendingRollbackError`) is retried `SYMBOL_LOCK_DB_RETRIES` times (default 1) after a rollback and a pause of `SYMBOL_LOCK_DB_RETRY_DELAY_S` (0.3 s) x attempt; only when the retry fails too does the gate fail closed as before (CRITICAL log, rate-limited Telegram, exits never gated). Programming errors are not retried; `SYMBOL_LOCK_DB_RETRIES=0` = the old behaviour; `SHARED_GATES_FAIL_CLOSED=false` still fails open. (2) a "table does not exist" error (ORA-00942 / Postgres UndefinedTable) re-creates `stockky_shared_symbol_lock` with `checkfirst` (never ALTERs; at most once per 5 min) before the retry. (3) the Telegram alert now carries the first line of the driver message (e.g. `DatabaseError: ORA-00942 ...`) so the cause is visible without opening the log. **Not changed:** `capital/shared_order_budget.py` and real-trade-service's copies of both gates have the same fail-closed shape and no retry yet. **If the alert keeps coming:** the retry only covers blips; read the new alert text or the `try_claim(...) failed (attempt N)` log line with its traceback for the ORA code (a permanent error such as a bad credential, a missing grant or a full tablespace needs fixing in the database, not in code). **Tests:** new `tests/test_e265_symbol_lock_retry.py` (12) pass here against the real `shared_symbol_lock.py` with stand-ins for sqlalchemy / models; the existing `tests/test_shared_symbol_lock.py` and the full suite were NOT run (sqlalchemy / pytest not installed here) - run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh position-stocks-service`. `docs/ENV_REFERENCE.txt` regenerated.

Cause (checked on the VM after the deploy, the Dhan list had loaded with 2,754 symbols): Dhan lists all four on NSE only in series **BE** (trade-for-trade), which this service does not trade (`_security_other_series`); the lookup ran only when the order was sent, after the symbol had been ENTER-approved. **Changes:** new `dhan_client.untradable_reason(db, symbol)` (same lookup as the order path; returns `NOT_TRADABLE_SERIES: ...` only when the list is loaded and the symbol is not in it; fails open when the list is empty or on any other error); `entry_engine/entry.py` Gate 4a WAITs such a candidate (REAL only, gate_tag `not_tradable`) before the circuit gate and before any order row; `swing/live.py` records the pick as SKIPPED and moves on to the next pick, so a slot is not spent on it. Switch `ENTRY_TRADABLE_GATE_ENABLED` (default true, in the control catalog; false = old behaviour). `get_security_id` and the order path are unchanged. BE stocks were not made tradable: they are surveillance / trade-for-trade names with thin liquidity and tight bands. **Not changed (found, not diagnosed):** the broker-stop SELL orders for KFINTECH (3) and NTPC (3) show CANCELLED with no event rows; NTPC's limits rise 302.4 -> 305.15 -> 306.35 -> 310.85, which looks like a trailing stop being replaced, not a fault. **Tests:** new `tests/test_e264_tradable_gate.py` (9: the helper on EQ / BE / unlisted / empty list / unexpected error, and the gate position in `entry.py` and `swing/live.py` checked by source order, not by running the full entry cycle) pass, run with stand-ins for sqlalchemy / httpx / pandas; `scripts/check_control_catalog.py` ok; the full suites were NOT run - run `bash scripts/docker_test.sh real-trade-service`. `docs/ENV_REFERENCE.txt` regenerated. **Also (VM run of the real-trade suite, 5,551 passed, 1 failed):** `tests/test_group322_no_new_silent_excepts.py` was already failing before E264 - E262's swing-probe skip in `portfolio/portfolio.py::import_broker_holdings` had a silent `except Exception: pass`; it now logs a warning and keeps the same fail-open behaviour (symbol is imported as before). VM check of E264: of 154 symbols with an ENTRY decision on 30 Oct, exactly 4 are not tradable (HFCL, STLTECH, TBZ, MOREPENLAB), so the gate would have blocked those four and nothing else.
- **E263 (30 Oct 2026): a flat Position Stocks trade is no longer closed at its 20 / 30-minute timer just because a candidate was refused for capital or the price made lower highs.** 30 Oct closed trades SATIN (30 min), RAMKY (24), CAMS (20 and 28), GOODLUCK: all `STAGNATION_EXIT`, -0.38 % .. +0.02 %, no stop or target near. Cause (code): E252's hold rule was defeated twice - each slot is capped at pool / `MAX_CONCURRENT_SCALP_POSITIONS` x `SCALP_SLOT_NOTIONAL_CAP_MULT` (30 % of the pool), so only about three of five slots can fill and `slot_pressure` (an `INSUFFICIENT_CAPITAL` skip in the last 10 min) was nearly always set; and `is_fading` treated any lower high over 10 minutes as fading, true in roughly half of all windows on a flat price. **Changes (position-stocks `orders/exit_adaptive.py`, call in `orders/eod_squareoff.py::run_stagnation_exit`):** a waiting candidate alone closes a flat trade only after `EXIT_SLOT_PRESSURE_MIN_AGE_MIN` (45; 0 = E252); `EXIT_FADE_STRICT` (on): fading needs the VWAP side missed by `EXIT_FADE_VWAP_MARGIN_PCT` (0.10), and lower highs count only while the trade is not in profit (`is_fading(..., entry=)`); fading still closes from the first timer, the stop, the 90-minute hard maximum and the EOD sweep are unchanged. Trade-off: a flat trade that turns into a loser can now reach its stop (~-Rs 24 on a ~Rs 2,000 slot) instead of being cut near -Rs 3. New read-only `scripts/movers_review.py` (asks `/scan/why/{symbol}` and `/scan/hits` for a symbol list and adds the price and day-move rules). **Findings, not changed:** of 26 Groww movers up >= 2 %, 7 are above `MAX_DAY_GAIN_PCT` (7) and 13 above the ~Rs 733 price a 3-share entry allows on a ~Rs 2,000 slot; run `scripts/gate_scorecard.py` before loosening anything. - **E262 (30 Oct 2026): the swing probe's 1 share is no longer adopted by the exit engine and broker_stop.** First live run of the E261 Forever Order probe (NTPC, 10:59 IST): BUY filled and the Forever Order was accepted and listed `PENDING` (id 22132610301931, trigger 300.95, limit 299.45; no TPIN needed at creation). Within about 5 minutes the log showed `import_broker_holdings: imported NTPC (1 shares @ avg 312.40) as a new tracked REAL position [same-day CNC delivery position]`, after which `execution/broker_stop.py` placed and re-placed its own DAY stops on the same share (tag BSTOP, triggers 302.40, 305.15, 306.35; two earlier ones cancelled) and the exit engine tracked a software stop and target for it. A second manager on a probe share can sell it before the next-morning check, and its DAY stops sit on the same share as the Forever Order. The ITC row seen the same morning (BSTOP stop at 09:15, trigger 257.45) is most likely the E132 probe's old share, adopted the same way. **Changes (real-trade-service):** (1) `swing/probe.py::is_probe_symbol(db, symbol)` - True while the open phase is working on the symbol (in-process `_ACTIVE` set, set after the symbol claim and cleared in a `finally`, because the BUY fills before the probe row is saved) or while the saved probe row is an open phase waiting for its check on that symbol; fail-open (any error = False). (2) `portfolio/portfolio.py::import_broker_holdings` skips such a symbol (logged: "skipped - it is the swing probe's 1 share"), before anything is created for it. (3) `check_phase` no longer sells the share when a tracked REAL position (OPEN / PARTIALLY_CLOSED / PENDING_EXIT) exists for it: the step line says the exit engine manages it; the PASS / FAIL verdict is about the stop order and does not depend on the share. **Not changed / not undone:** the NTPC and ITC positions that are already tracked stay managed by the exit engine (nothing here closes or cancels them); an already tracked probe share still gets BSTOP DAY stops. `broker_stop.py` and the swing lane still place DAY stops; this release does not wire Forever Orders into the lane. **Tests:** `tests/test_swing_lane.py` (2 new: `is_probe_symbol` fail-open and in-process path, and the shape of the import and check-phase guards; 35 tests in the file pass, run without pytest). Not run: the full real-trade suite and a live import cycle (SQLAlchemy not installed here) - run `python -m pytest tests -q` in `services/real-trade-service` on the VM. This zip also contains E261.
- **E261 (30 Oct 2026): the swing probe FAILed because it tested an order type that cannot survive the night; it now tests Dhan's Forever Order (GTT).** 30 Oct 10:51 IST `dhan_swing_probe` (phase status) showed `FAIL - the stop order is no longer in Dhan's order book: it did not survive the night`. Cause (code + Dhan docs): the probe placed a normal `STOP_LOSS` with `validity DAY` and read it back from `get_order_list`, which is today's order book only; Dhan drops a DAY order at the close, so the FAIL was the expected result, not a broker fault, and the same applies to `execution/broker_stop.py` (it also places DAY stops), which is why position-stocks re-arms its overnight stop every morning. **Keep `SWING_BROKER_STOP_ENABLED` off.** Dhan's overnight-capable order is the Forever Order (`POST /v2/forever/orders`, `orderFlag SINGLE`, CNC, listed by `GET /v2/forever/all`, cancelled by `DELETE /v2/forever/orders/{id}`). **Changes (real-trade-service only):** (1) `execution/dhan_client.py`: `place_forever_order()` (armed only, tick-rounded, direct REST like `ddpi_status`), `get_forever_orders()` (tries `/forever/all`, then `/forever/orders`, since Dhan's docs show both), `cancel_forever_order()`. (2) `swing/probe.py`: new `kind` (`forever` default, `day` = the old stop as a control); open phase places a 1-share CNC BUY then a SELL forever order 4% below with a limit 0.5% under the trigger, reads it back from the forever list; check phase (next trading morning) reads the same list, PASS only for PENDING / CONFIRM / TRANSIT, cancels it and sells the share; a probe saved before this release (no `kind`) is judged as a DAY stop. `judge_check(row, kind)` now explains that a missing DAY order is expected. The check phase now also releases the probe's symbol lock when its cleanup fails, and stores its step lines (`check_steps`) so `phase=status` can show whether the share was sold or needs the CDSL TPIN. (3) `POST /swing/probe` takes `kind`; `scripts/dhan_swing_probe.py --kind forever|day`; runner job `dhan_swing_probe` has a Stop type choice (default forever). **Not changed:** `execution/broker_stop.py` and the swing lane still use DAY stops; wiring a Forever Order into the lane is a separate step and only after this probe PASSes. **Not verified (no live call here):** the Forever Order request fields follow Dhan's published docs; whether Dhan needs the CDSL TPIN at creation for a CNC SELL, and what `orderStatus` a waiting SINGLE order shows on this account (docs list PENDING and CONFIRM), will only show in the open run. Tests: `tests/test_swing_lane.py` (3 new, all pass; run without pytest here), runner job argument check and script CLI check; calibration-runner tests that touch jobs (8 files) pass; `check_control_catalog.py` ok. Not run: the full real-trade suite (SQLAlchemy not installed in this sandbox) - run `python -m pytest tests -q` in `services/real-trade-service` on the VM.
- **E260 (30 Oct 2026): HFCL "No Dhan NSE_EQ security_id" - blank-series rows no longer dropped, the miss now says why and re-loads the list once; DDPI-aware eDIS check and KFINTECH-style exit alert.** (1) Alert "Auto BUY rejected by Dhan - HFCL: No Dhan NSE_EQ security_id found" has NO "list is empty" suffix, so the Dhan list HAD loaded and HFCL was simply not in it. Code finding (`execution/dhan_client.py`, both services): the SDK path reads the list through pandas, where an empty `SEM_SERIES` is NaN and `str(nan)` is `"nan"`; the filter accepts only `"EQ"` or `""`, so any NSE equity row with an empty series was silently dropped (the CSV fallback path already accepted empty). New `_norm_series()` treats nan/none/null as empty. Not proven to be HFCL's cause - that needs the real row (command in the reply): if HFCL is listed in series BE or similar it is trade-for-trade and correctly not tradable. The loader now also remembers symbols listed only outside EQ, so the error reads "Dhan lists it on NSE only in series 'BE' ... not tradable here" or "not in Dhan's NSE equity list (N symbols, checked again just now)". On a miss with a loaded cache, ONE forced re-load runs (throttled to once per 30 min, `_SECURITY_MISS_RELOAD_MIN_S`) in case the 24 h cache lags. No filter was loosened beyond the blank-series rule the code already meant. (2) KFINTECH x3 stop_hit alert (CDSL / TPIN) and DDPI: new `dhan_client.ddpi_status()` reads Dhan's `GET /v2/profile` field `ddpi` (cached 1 h; unknown = never treated as active; NOT yet confirmed against a live response from this account) and `edis_verification_summary()` (both services) returns `verified_today: true, ddpi_active: true` without needing per-holding TPIN rows when DDPI is Active, so the daily check and hourly reconcile stop reporting holdings as pending. The real-trade exit alert says "CDSL refusal although DDPI is Active" (with the likely causes) instead of telling you to enter a TPIN when Dhan's profile shows DDPI Active. New admin route `GET /dhan/ddpi` (real-trade). Tests: new `tests/test_e259_security_ddpi.py` in real-trade (13) and position-stocks (12); full suites: real-trade 5533 passed, 2 skipped, 4 failed (`test_oracle_compat`, the `oracledb` driver is not installed in the sandbox); position-stocks 4567 passed, 2 skipped, 1 failed (`test_dhan_client::test_valid_creds_returns_client`, `dhanhq` SDK not installed in the sandbox). Not run: a live Dhan call. This zip also contains E259.
- **E259 (30 Oct 2026): Top picks cards show a price and levels again, the card says WHY the opening gate refused a pick, and gate-refused names no longer fill the shortlist slots.** 30 Oct 09:40-09:46 IST VM output: `/top-picks` returned `ltp: null` for ALIVUS / TRENT / SANGHVIMOV (all "-"), although `/quote/TRENT` (Dhan, age 0 s), `/board/status` (499 symbols, 0 errors) and `/board/<symbol>` all worked - **not an API fault**. Cause: the card took its price from the ranked board list, and `ms.rank_rows` drops every row with score <= 0; a stock that gapped up and then chopped (strength `clean: false`) has score 0, so it was never listed. Second finding: every cycle's 5 shortlist slots were gap-up names (TRENT +5.46 %, ALIVUS +3.84 %, SANGHVIMOV +3.17 %, BOROLTD +7 %, KRISHNADEF +3.45 %; limit +3 %) that `screening/opening_gate.py` refuses by design, so nothing else was tried; the card only said "opening gate" because `main.py` kept the text before the first colon. **Changes (position-stocks only):** (1) `screening/top_picks.py` - `row_from_symbol_view()` + `build(..., fallback=)`: a fresh engine pick with no ranked board row gets its price, change %, zone score, stop / target / R:R and spark from `board.symbol_view()` (flag `off_board`, reason "not on the ranked board"); `GET /top-picks` passes the fallback. It never lets a name through that the gate would refuse and changes no threshold; outside 09:15-10:00 nothing is held. Held names are logged ("held out of the shortlist") and listed in `GET /shortlist` as `held_by_gate`. Switch: `SHORTLIST_GATE_PREFILTER` (default 1; 0 = old behaviour). Not changed: `OPENING_GATE_MAX_GAP_UP_PCT` (3.0) and the other gate limits (they come from your backtest; loosening needs data first); real-trade-service's own `/top-picks/{mode}` still uses the ranked board list only. - **E258 (29 Oct 2026): a protective SELL stop is kept inside the exchange circuit band (ITC overnight stop rejected, carry fell back to a square-off).** Dhan RMS refused ITC's overnight stop with `Rate Not Within Ckt Limit 257.80 To 273.70`: the stop is 4% below entry (266.8 x 0.96 = 256.15) but ITC's band was only about +-3%, so the trigger lay below the lower circuit; `_place_overnight_stop` then returned None and the position was squared off instead of carried. **Changes:** (1) `position-stocks-service` `orders/circuit_gate.py`: new `parse_band()` (band from Dhan's rejection text), `clamp_sell_stop()` (trigger raised to lower circuit + 1 tick; None when the stock sits on its lower circuit) and `band_for()` (band and price from the cached /quote row, fails open). `orders/eod_squareoff._place_overnight_stop` raises the trigger before sending when the band is known, and when it is not, retries ONCE with the band named in Dhan's circuit rejection; any other error is not retried. `overnight_stop.rearm` uses the same function, so the morning re-arm is covered too. (2) `real-trade-service` `execution/broker_stop.py` had the same gap and, worse, no backoff: `trigger_for(stop, ltp, lower=None)` now raises the trigger to lower circuit + 1 tick (lower circuit from the live tick, else the band learned from a rejection), a circuit rejection remembers the band so the next pass sends a trigger inside it, and a repeat refusal backs the symbol off (BROKER_STOP_EDIS_BACKOFF_S) instead of re-sending every sync pass. No new switch: this only moves a trigger that Dhan would refuse anyway. The stop is closer than 4% for such stocks (it cannot rest below the band); the 'stop level' shown in the carry Telegram summary is still the unclamped 4% figure. **Tests:** position-stocks `tests/test_e258_overnight_stop_circuit_band.py` (8, pass here against the real code with stand-ins for the missing libraries); real-trade `tests/test_e258_broker_stop_circuit_band.py` (5: 2 pure ones pass here, the 3 sync tests need sqlalchemy and are NOT yet run); `tests/conftest.py` (position-stocks) switches the band lookup off for older tests so none makes an HTTP call. Run the full position-stocks and real-trade suites on the VM.**

- **E252 (28 Oct 2026): adaptive trailing stop; flat trades closed only when fading or a slot is wanted; day-spike guard and range check in real-trade-service; exit follow-up study.** SYNGENE 28 Oct: bought 389.45, the trail started at a fixed +1.0 % and sat a fixed 0.78 % under the peak (0.6 x its 1.3 % stop), exited 391.15 (+0.44 %), then the stock ran to 412.7 (target 399.20). HEGAM: closed by the 20-minute no-follow-through rule at -0.21 %; it was fading, so the exit was right, but the rule cannot tell fading from coiling and fires even when no candidate waits. **position-stocks** (`orders/exit_adaptive.py`, wired into `orders/trailing.py` and `orders/eod_squareoff.py::run_stagnation_exit`): (1) trail activation = the later of `TRAIL_ACTIVATE_PCT` and `TRAIL_ACTIVATE_TARGET_FRAC` (0.5) x the trade's own target %, capped `TRAIL_ACTIVATE_MAX_PCT` (2.0); trail distance = the larger of the old distance and `TRAIL_BAR_RANGE_MULT` (1.0) x the stock's average 5-minute bar range (1-minute bar store, tick buffer as fallback), never wider than `TRAIL_MAX_DIST_STOP_FRAC` (1.0) x its stop; breakeven + ticks stays the first lock; shorts mirror it; switch `TRAIL_ADAPTIVE_ENABLED`. (2) no-follow-through gain = `NFT_TARGET_FRAC` (0.2) x target %, clamped 0.4-0.8 %; flat band = `STAGNATION_BAND_TARGET_FRAC` (0.15) x target %, clamped 0.25-0.6 %. (3) a flagged trade is closed only when it is fading (wrong side of the day VWAP, or lower highs / a short's higher lows over `EXIT_FADE_WINDOW_MIN` 10 minutes) or a candidate was skipped for `INSUFFICIENT_CAPITAL` / `MAX_POSITIONS` in the last `EXIT_SLOT_PRESSURE_WINDOW_MIN` (10) minutes (read from `scalp_candidate_log`); otherwise it is left to stop, target or the EOD sweep, with `EXIT_HARD_MAX_MINUTES` (90) as a hard maximum holding time; unknown fade data = hold. Switch `EXIT_ADAPTIVE_ENABLED`; false = the old fixed numbers and always close. Both switches are on the Calibration page (shipped true). The "held" line is logged once per position. **real-trade-service** (`entry_engine/day_spike_gate.py`, Gate 4d in `entry_engine/entry.py`): day-spike guard `SPIKE_GUARD_MODE` (up >= 2 x the stock's daily ATR %, clamped 2.5-6 %, AND range position >= 0.85 -> WAIT, gate tag `spike_chase`) and range-used-up check `GAIN_ROOM_MODE` (room to today's low x (1 + 2 x daily ATR %) under half the target % -> WAIT, tag `gain_room`); live | log | off, fail open. Not ported: the stop widening and the reclaim re-entry (a real-trade stop-out already blocks a same-day re-buy), and the tick-level thrust read (no tick buffer there). Not built: the near-high stop shrink, waiting on the data below. `scripts/exit_followup_study.py` (read-only): D1 - for every STAGNATION_EXIT / trailing-stop exit, the best price in the next 30 and 60 minutes from the recorded 1-minute bars and whether the target or the stop came first; D2 - REAL stop-outs closed within N minutes of entry. Run it on the VM, then set the numbers above from it. **Tests that ran:** `tests/test_e252_adaptive_exits.py` (11) and real-trade `tests/test_e252_day_spike_gate.py` (6) and `scripts/test_exit_followup_study.py` (4) pass, run without the database layer (this workspace cannot install packages); both conftests switch the new rules off for older tests. **Tests that did NOT run:** the full position-stocks and real-trade suites, the catalog coverage check and the repo script checks - run them on the VM.
- **E251 (28 Oct 2026): wider, volatility-aware stop; no market buy at the top of a spike; entries need real room to gain; one reclaim re-entry after a stop-out.** ARVIND 28 Oct: bought 518.15 at 12:26 right after a ~6 % day spike, the 1.2 % stop (6.4) was hit at 12:27, the stock then ran to ~530 (the 2.5 % target). Of 14 position-stocks trades since 26 Oct, 11 sat exactly on the 0.8 % stop floor and several were stopped in 2-12 minutes. (1) **Stop:** `ADAPTIVE_BAR_STOP_MIN_PCT` 0.8 -> 1.2, `SCALP_TARGET_CAP_PCT` 2.5 -> 3.0, `SCALP_TARGET_CAP_MIN_STOP_PCT` 0.8 -> 1.2, `REARM_MIN_STOP_PCT` 0.7 -> 1.0; new `orders/adaptive.py::apply_stop_floor` keeps a long's stop at least `ADAPTIVE_STOP_DAILY_ATR_FRAC` (0.45) x the stock's own daily ATR % and `ADAPTIVE_STOP_M1_MULT` (2.0) x its average 1-minute range over the last 5 minutes, capped at `ADAPTIVE_STOP_FLOOR_MAX_PCT` (1.5); the target moves with it (2:1); `stop_basis` gets a `+f` tag. Position size is risk-based (risk / stop), so a wider stop means fewer shares, not a bigger rupee loss; `RISK_PER_TRADE_PCT` is untouched. (2) **Entry timing:** new `orders/entry_timing.py` (`SPIKE_GUARD_MODE` live): no MARKET buy when the stock is up >= its own limit on the day (1.2 x daily ATR %, clamped 2.5-6 %, 4 % when unknown) AND in the top 15 % of today's range, or is up >= its own limit inside 3 minutes (3 x its average 1-minute range, clamped 1-2.5 %) AND within 0.25 % of that window's high. The refusal is `SPIKE_CHASE:...`, armed for the existing pullback re-arm so the stock is bought after a real pullback and bounce; per-symbol skip, the next shortlisted pick is still tried. (3) **Gain room:** new `orders/gain_room.py` (`GAIN_ROOM_MODE` live): replays the final stop % / target % on the stock's own 1-minute bars today (existing `trade_odds.py`) and refuses when the target came first 8+ points less often than break-even needs (25+ resolved replays), or when the stock has used up ~2 x its normal daily range so less than half the target is left above the price. Both fail open on missing data. (4) **Reclaim re-entry:** `orders/stopout_guard.py::reclaim_decision` / `reclaim_allowed` (`REENTRY_RECLAIM_ENABLED`): a symbol whose only trade today was one losing stop-out may be bought once more 2 min - 2 h later when price is back above the old entry (+0.1 %) and not past half way to the old target; lifts only the symbol block and the loss-today / cooldown re-entry guards, the 3-stop-out day pause and every other gate still apply. Not built: a stop "grace" window (the stop is a broker-side Super Order leg and cannot ignore a wick without losing protection; the wider stop and the spike guard cover it). Nothing is tuned to one stock: every limit is a multiple of the stock's own ATR / 1-minute range with a clamp. Switches on the Real Money Calibration page: `ADAPTIVE_STOP_FLOOR_ENABLED`, `ADAPTIVE_STOP_DAILY_ATR_FRAC`, `ADAPTIVE_STOP_M1_MULT`, `ADAPTIVE_STOP_FLOOR_MAX_PCT`, `SPIKE_GUARD_MODE`, `GAIN_ROOM_MODE`, `REENTRY_RECLAIM_ENABLED`. Tests: `tests/test_e251_stop_floor_spike_gain_room_reclaim.py` (25) pass; three older tests that pinned the old defaults were updated (test_group391_opening_plan, test_group479_guards, test_pullback_rearm); `scripts/check_control_catalog.py` ok; `docs/ENV_REFERENCE.txt` regenerated. NOT run here: the tests that need SQLAlchemy (no PyPI access in this session) - run the full suite on the VM. All thresholds are first values from the code and one day's trades, not calibrated on a larger sample.
- **E250 (28 Oct 2026): SELL probe no longer false-FAILs a late cover.** `orders/sell_probe.py`: the cover confirmation waits `poll_s x COVER_WAIT_MULT` (20s -> 60s) and takes one grace look (3s pause, order list + Dhan positions) before giving up. ONGC 28 Oct 11:10: the cover filled just after the 20s wait, the probe verdict became FAIL and blocked live shorts (`SHORT_PROBE_NOT_PASSED`) even though two clean round trips preceded it. Test: `test_a_cover_that_fills_after_the_old_wait_is_still_a_pass`. Entry/legs/adopt logic unchanged; short band and gates unchanged (config only).
- E249 (2026-10-28): position-stocks: a rank-1 stock that fails a per-symbol sizing / level check no longer ends the whole cycle, and the day-mover scan no longer lists moves entry always refuses. Read from `latest_results_rt/ps.zip` + the screenshots (Groww MTF list, 12:15 IST). **Bug 1:** `orders/entry.py::_SYMBOL_SPECIFIC_SKIP_PREFIXES` lacked `MIN_QTY`, `RR_FLOOR`, `STOP_ATR_FLOOR`, `MIN_PRICE`, so `main.py` treated them as global and broke out of the shortlist loop, and `_GATE_COOLDOWN_PREFIXES` lacked them too, so the same name came back as rank 1 every ~15 s. `_unaffordable_filter` only compares ONE share with the pool's free cash, not with slot / `ENTRY_MIN_QTY`, so any stock priced above roughly slot/3 (recorded slots are Rs 1,600-2,900 over 15 trades) reached the quality gate, hit `MIN_QTY` and blocked every cheaper passer behind it. Fix: the four prefixes fall through and start the existing 60 s cooldown (`GATE_REJECT_COOLDOWN_S`); global reasons (armed, kill switch, order budget, max positions, quality gate) still end the cycle. **Bug 2:** the scan listed day movers up to `DAY_MOVER_MAX_PCT` 20 % (short 20 %) while entry refuses a long above `MAX_DAY_GAIN_PCT` 7 % and a short below `SHORT_MAX_DAY_FALL_PCT` 5 %, so +15 % names used quality-gate slots to be refused later. New `engine._day_mover_max(side)` = min of the two (guard 0 = off); `/scan/why/{symbol}` now says "beyond the entry guard" instead of "qualifies as a day mover". What can be bought is unchanged (same previous close feeds both checks). Tests: new tests/test_e249_fallthrough_and_daymover_cap.py (8), tests/test_e232 short day-mover test moved from a 6 % to a 4.5 % faller; position-stocks full suite 4492 passed. Not run: live feed, real-trade suite (no change there). Not changed: real-trade candidate funnel, SELL probe verdict (re-run the probe after deploying E244/E246).
- E248 (2026-10-28): why nothing is bought or sold from 09:15, why so few opening orders go out, and a read-only study of the opening move on your own bars. **No trading rule changed.** Reading the code (position-stocks, no live logs in the zip): (1) the entry window itself opens at 09:15 (`ENTRY_NO_BEFORE_IST`, `OPENING_GATE_MIN_MINUTES_AFTER_OPEN` 0), so the clock is not the blocker. (2) The scan cannot list a normal candidate before about 09:20: `_rolling_pct_change` needs a tick at least one window old, the 1 and 15 minute windows are off (`DISABLED_SCAN_WINDOWS` 1,15), so the first rolling candidate needs the 5-minute window (+1.0 %) and five minutes of ticks. The only 09:15 path is the day-mover (`DAY_MOVER_MIN_PCT` 4 %), and a stock already 4 % up at the open is a gap that the opening gate (`OPENING_GATE_MAX_GAP_UP_PCT` 3 %) and the pre-open gap block (`PREOPEN_GAP_UP_BLOCK_PCT` 6 %, 1.5 x ATR) refuse until 10:00, so the two rules cancel each other at the bell. (3) After that every automatic entry in 09:15-10:00 must pass, in order: pre-open gap, opening gate (fails closed: previous day closed in the upper half, price at/above open and previous close, opening range built after 09:30, Nifty), quality gate, then sizing: half size until 09:45 (`OPENING_SIZE_MULT` 0.5) and `ENTRY_MIN_QTY` 3, so a stock whose price is more than a third of the half-size slot is skipped as MIN_QTY (on about Rs 18k capital this removes most mid and high priced names); two stop-outs pause entries until 09:45 and three pause the day. Caps: 5 open positions, 10 entries a day. (4) SELL: automatic shorts start at 09:45 by design (`SHORT_ENTRY_START_IST`, the day VWAP is thin before that) and `SHORT_MAX_POSITIONS` is 2; exits of held longs are not time-gated. (5) The 09:10 pre-open snapshot (`screening/preopen.py`) is used only as a BLOCKER for big gaps, never to choose or rank a buy or sell; a real NSE pre-open order book (indicative equilibrium price, buy/sell quantities) is still not read by any service. NSE facts used (NSE and broker pages): order entry 09:00-09:08, matching 09:08-09:12, buffer 09:12-09:15, the equilibrium price is the day open. Research on opening-range breakouts with high-volume stocks (Zarattini, Barbon, Aziz) is on US stocks 2016-2023, not NSE, so it is a reason to measure, not proof. This repo's own 14-day breakdown (group 268: entries before 10:30 won 1 of 10) and the September REAL split (09-10 h entries lost most) point the other way, which is why the gates were not loosened without a measurement. **New `scripts/preopen_momentum_study.py`** (read-only, stdlib, `python3 scripts/preopen_momentum_study.py BAR_DIR [--days 20] [--out DIR] [--candidate-log cand.json]`): on the recorded 1-minute bars it measures, per gap bucket and exit time (09:45, 10:00, 10:30, 15:00), A_follow (buy/sell at the 09:15 open in the direction of the pre-open gap), A_fade (the opposite), B_5m (side of the first five minutes, entry 09:20), C_agree (gap and first five minutes agree) and ORB5 (opening-range breakout, stop at the other end of the range), net of `--cost-pct` (0.15), with a 95 % interval built on daily means and a verdict that needs `--min-n` 30 and `--min-days` 10. With `--candidate-log` (from `/candidates/log?limit=500`) it also counts the 09:15-10:00 candidate rows by skipping gate and entries by half hour. Tests: scripts/test_preopen_momentum_study.py 11 passed (stand-in runner, pytest not installed here); one measured number checked against an independent calculation on random-walk bars. Not run: any service suite (no service code changed), real bars (none in the zip). Next step: copy the `bar-records` volume out, run the study over the latest 20 dates, and paste the A_follow vs B_5m tables; only a bucket with verdict `edge` over enough days gets a small switchable live change.
- E247 (2026-10-28): analysis-intelligence event tracker caps concurrent Yahoo calls. The 06:02 IST restart log showed "Connection pool is full, discarding connection: query2.finance.yahoo.com. Connection pool size: 10" more than 20 times in 30 s: the quality gate and the /check warm-up start about 10 symbols together and each makes several yfinance calls at once, so the 10-connection pool overflowed and each overflow was a wasted reconnect on a service that was already slow (the first event answers then arrived after the position-stocks gate had given up, which is the burst of EVENT_CHECK_UNAVAILABLE skips; those retry next cycle and the late answers are kept, unchanged). `event/main.py`: new `_yf_slot()`, a bounded semaphore (`EVENT_YF_MAX_CONCURRENT`, default 6, 0 = no cap) taken around every cache-missing `cached_yf` call and the company-name `ticker.info` call; a caller waits at most `EVENT_YF_SLOT_WAIT_S` (default 20) for a slot and then runs anyway, so a slot can never block for good; cache hits take no slot. What is fetched and returned is unchanged. Not changed: the quality-gate wait times, and the one-off live_quotes write timeout (single occurrence). Tests: analysis-intelligence full suite 2647 passed (4 new in tests/test_e247_yf_slots.py); repo script tests 16 passed; `docs/ENV_REFERENCE.txt` regenerated (1,518 variables) and `docs/FILE_MANIFEST.txt` refreshed. Not run: live Yahoo.
- E246 (2026-10-28): an ONGC SELL-probe short that Dhan had already covered no longer stays OPEN in Stockky, plus the two red CI checks. **Bug:** at 11:10:43 the probe's 1-share SELL filled (223.83) and its cover BUY (id 321261028615307) was "not confirmed filled within 20s", because the cover was only looked for in Dhan's order list. The probe then adopted the short as a normal row (`window_source PROBE`, status EXIT_LEGS_REJECTED, Cover -), while Dhan's positions already showed ONGC closed (sold 223.83, bought 223.85, net 0). The probe had cancelled that row's own Super Order legs, so the leg-based reconcile could never see an exit, and a later Exit Now or the 14:55 cover would have BUY-ed a share that is not short, opening a real LONG. **Fix (`orders/sell_probe.py`):** the cover wait now also reads Dhan's POSITIONS (every 2nd second and at the end); flat beyond the baseline with a cover placed counts as covered (price = position buyAvg, else the reference), and a last positions look runs before any row is adopted. An adopted row keeps the cover order id in `dhan_exit_order_id`. **Heal (`orders/reconcile.py`, new `heal_covered_probe_shorts`, first step of `run_exit_reconciliation`, 15 s throttle):** a PROBE SHORT row that is OPEN or EXIT_LEGS_REJECTED is closed (status MANUAL_EXIT) when Dhan's positions are readable, hold a row for the security and show nothing open on it; exit price = the cover order's own fill (by `dhan_exit_order_id`, else a traded SELLPROBE_COVER BUY), else the position's buyAvg; P&L uses the short sign (ONGC: -0.02). Unreadable positions, no row, an open delivery row, no readable price, or any non-probe short: row left alone. A probe never reserved ledger capital, so none is released. The existing ONGC row heals on the first reconcile after deploy (or press Check Exits Now). **Guard (`orders/eod_squareoff.py::_fire_flat_sell`):** a PROBE short is healed first and, if it was already covered, `PositionAlreadyFlat` is raised so no cover BUY is sent. **CI:** `tests/test_short_foundation.py` failed because `main.py` read `SHORT_MODE` for the SELL orders route; it now uses `short_entry.cfg_mode()` (same off/shadow/live value), so only the allowed modules read it. `frontend/src/appHelpers.test.mjs` failed on `TabButton.tsx deleted`: `components/app/TabButton.tsx` and `BackendStatusDot.tsx` (E70 removed them, nothing imports them) were back in the tree; deleted again. Tests: position-stocks full suite 4480 passed (14 new: 12 in tests/test_e246_probe_short_heal.py, 2 in tests/test_sell_probe.py with a fake Dhan that fills the cover but hides it from the order list); frontend `npm test` all 45 test files pass; gen_env_reference --check, check_merge_conflicts clean; `docs/FILE_MANIFEST.txt` regenerated. Not run: real Dhan.

- E137 (2026-10-25): swing lane: a stock with a stale last bar is never picked. Bug (reading `swing/engine.py` and `ranker.py`):** the snapshot's bar date is the newest bar of ANY stock. A stock that is halted, suspended or has stale data keeps its old last bar (days old) but still gets a rank, a `screen` flag and an ATR. It could be PLANNED as a pick. In shadow it was then SKIPPED at settle ("no bar for the entry session"), wasting one of the day's pick slots; in `live` the morning entry reads the PLANNED rows and would try to buy it on the strength of old prices.
- E136 (2026-10-25): S4 probe: two bugs found reading `swing/probe.py`. 1. **A failed open phase blocked the probe forever.** `open_phase` refused to run while the last probe row was "an open phase without `checked`". A FAILED open (BUY not filled, or Dhan refused the order or the stop) is saved without a `checked` flag and without a stop id, so every later `open` said "waiting for its check phase", while `check` said "no open phase is waiting". No way out except editing the database. Now `probe.waiting_for_check(prev)` is true only for an open phase that placed a stop (has `stop_id`) ...
- E135 (2026-10-25): swing report: count only picks signalled since a date. Follow-up to E134. When the stock list changes (built-in -> `SWING_UNIVERSE_SYMBOLS`), the shadow ledger mixes two universes. `GET /swing/report?since=YYYY-MM-DD` now counts only trades whose signal day is on or after that date (both the closed-trade verdict and `by_status`); the answer echoes `since`. A bad date returns an `error`. Without `since` nothing changes.
- E134 (2026-10-25): swing lane: every scan records which stock list it ranked. Follow-up to E133. The built-in 118-name list and your own `SWING_UNIVERSE_SYMBOLS` list give different evidence, but nothing marked which scan used which.

New table `scalp_trade_journal` (own snapshot of the trade, so notes survive the 3-day retention cleanup), `orders/journal_notes.py`, routes `GET /journal/notes` and `PUT /journal/notes/{position_id}` (admin), optional `reason` / `emotion` on `POST /positions/manual/buy` (validated before the order; a note failure never fails a placed buy). Frontend: Feeling + Why boxes on Manual buy, and Board tab cards *Win rate by feeling* and *Trade notes*. Descriptive only, nothing gates on it. Tests: 12 backend (`tests/test_e30_journal_notes.py`), 2 frontend node tests (`journalNotesView.test.mjs`). Doc: `docs/E30_JOURNAL_NOTES.md`.
- E36 (E29, pattern alerts): one daily digest (after the close, `PATTERN_ALERTS_TIME` 16:05 IST, weekdays) of fresh chart signals on the WATCHLIST: confirmed breakout / breakdown, golden / death cross (last 2 daily bars), Bollinger squeeze on / released. Alert only, nothing trades on it, and the message says the signals are unproven. A signal already sent is not repeated for 3-10 days (state in `/data/pattern_alerts_state.json`, marked only after the notification service accepted the digest). New `scheduler/pattern_alerts.py`, `GET /scheduler/pattern-alerts/status`, `POST /scheduler/pattern-alerts/run?dry_run=true`. `chart_context` gained an uncapped additive `events` list (the 14-badge cap could hide a cross or squeeze on a crowded chart). 11 scheduler tests + 1 shared test (+ generated copy). Doc: `docs/E29_PATTERN_ALERTS.md`.
- E35 (C21, log-only pattern logging): the C18-C20 pattern signals now get their own fixed feature row (`PATTERN_COLUMNS`, 23 `pt_*` columns, own hash) and a shadow evidence record (`scoring.pattern_shadow`, five separate groups, `in_tqs: false`), both returned by `analyze_extras()`. `FEATURE_COLUMNS_V2` and `technical_quality_score` are unchanged (tested); no gate, score or model reads the new rows. Copies re-synced; 8 new tests (+ generated service copy). A7 re-checked: the general 14:00 entry cutoff already exists (groups 470 / 479). Doc: `docs/C21_PATTERN_LOGGING.md`.
- E27 (candles on every timeframe): the candle chart now supports 1m, 5m, 15m, 30m, 1h and daily, backend and frontend. New `shared/market_structure/timeframes.py` (session-aligned buckets, closed vs forming candle, IST dates, session-break rule for 2-3 candle patterns), `chart_context(tf=...)`, technical `/chart-context/{sym}?tf=` (daily path unchanged; intraday misses answer 200 + reason), gateway `?tf=` with its own `technical_chart` breaker, market-data `/history` intraday row cap (`MAX_HISTORY_ROWS_INTRADAY`, default 2600; the 260 cap cut a 1-minute chart to 260 minutes). Frontend: timeframe selector + per-timeframe zoom, time axis, session separators, hollow forming candle, auto-refresh in market hours. 40 new Python tests (plus generated service copies) + E27 block in `chartContext.test.mjs`. Doc: `docs/E27_CANDLE_TIMEFRAMES.md`.

## Earlier groups and dated entries (as kept in the old CHANGELOG_INDEX.md)

- 2026-10-22 (entry live anchor): position-stocks now moves the stop / target legs to the freshest live tick at order time (same stop % / target %), instead of the scan price. IONEXCHANG: signal 354.60, fill 352.30, stop 0.15 % under the fill, out in 21 s. `ENTRY_ANCHOR_TO_LIVE=0` = old behaviour. New `orders/live_anchor.py`, catalog flag, autocalib-protected, 7 tests. Doc: `docs/ENTRY_LIVE_ANCHOR_2026-10-22.md`.
- group491 (atomic file): an interrupted walk-forward no longer empties `backtest.json`. `vm_calibrate.sh` and the runner's `out_file` jobs now write `<file>.part` and replace the saved file only when the command finished, so a runner restart mid-run (21 Oct `calibration_full`, cut off at 15/355 symbols) keeps the last good `backtest.json` instead of leaving it empty. 3 new tests. Doc: `docs/GROUP491_ATOMIC_BACKTEST_JSON.md`.
- group491 (resume): the 2-hour structure walk-forward (`scripts/structure_backtest.py`) now saves each finished symbol (`--checkpoint`) and continues a killed / timed-out run (`--resume`), with `--jobs N`; runner job `structure_backtest` is resumable (Resume button, `ckpt/structure_<seed>`) and `vm_calibrate.sh` passes the flags (`RESUME=1`). Horizon / min-n / thresholds changes reuse the saved symbols; a changed history file, step, entry or costs recompute. +7 tests, group466 test updated. Doc: `docs/GROUP491_STRUCTURE_WALKFORWARD_RESUME.md`.
- group490: CI + runner fixes. (1) `RT_BREAKER_PROBE_ALERT_REPEAT_S` read was not blank-safe (failed `test_env_numeric_sweep`); fixed + test. (2) circuit_breaker shared-copy drift accepted in the baseline on purpose (only real-trade alerts via Telegram). (3) `calibration_pack.py` crashed with a JSONDecodeError on an empty `backtest.json` (a walk-forward that did not finish): the script now ends with a clear line and the runner reports an empty `.json` input as "needs data first"; +5 tests. (4) calibration-runner `test_every_rule_points_at_a_real_switch...` (failing since group 481) now accepts protected keys read from service config. Doc: `docs/GROUP490_CI_AND_PACK_FIXES.md`.
- group489: audit fix to group487. The small-leftover gate left out every leftover without a recent cached price, including symbols missing from Dhan's scrip map that only yfinance can price (they would never have been priced through `/quotes/bulk` again). Those now keep the yfinance path (`_dhan_can_price`, fails safe to the old path); only symbols Dhan knows but answered late are skipped. +3 tests (16 in `test_group487_bulk_small_leftover.py`). Doc: `docs/GROUP487_BULK_SMALL_LEFTOVER.md`.
- group488: position-stocks' first background refresh of a stale scrip-master snapshot now waits `ANGELONE_SCRIP_MASTER_BG_START_DELAY_S` (default 30 s + up to 50 % jitter from process start) so it does not hit AngelOne's file server together with market-data-service after a compose up; cold starts are not delayed. The snapshot volumes (`scrip-cache-md`, `scrip-cache-ps`) were already in compose. 8 new tests. Doc: `docs/GROUP488_SCRIP_MASTER_STAGGER.md`.
- group487: `/quotes/bulk` sent the few symbols Dhan could not price to `yf.download` (18 s hard timeout, then reported as a 502 rate-limit event). When Dhan priced >= 90 % of the request and <= 8 symbols are left, those get a cached price (<= 180 s, tagged `stale_leftover(<source>)`) or are left out, with no yfinance call; indices and held symbols keep the old path. A yfinance hard timeout / open circuit is no longer reported as a rate limit (real 429 still is). `QUOTE_BULK_SMALL_LEFTOVER_SKIP=0` restores the old behaviour. 13 new tests; market-data suite 1726 passed. Doc: `docs/GROUP487_BULK_SMALL_LEFTOVER.md`.
- group485: real-trade circuit breaker sent a Telegram "probe failed again" every 120 s for the whole outage (58 in a row on 2026-10-21). First failed probe still alerts; repeats now once per `RT_BREAKER_PROBE_ALERT_REPEAT_S` (default 1800, 0 = old behaviour); alerts and `/resilience/status` now include `last_error`. 26 breaker tests pass; ENV_REFERENCE regenerated. Doc: `docs/GROUP485_RT_BREAKER_ALERT_THROTTLE.md`.
- group470: fixes from the 19-20 Oct runner outputs (no gate behavior changed). (1) a full admin JWT was printed in `calibration_full` output: the redactor now hides any JWT, `--token VALUE` and `TOKEN=` values. (2) `gate_check` and `calibration_pack` failed with exit 2: `--ab` is now repeated per file (`globflag:` argv token). (3) `calibration_snapshot` / checkpointed jobs reported `input missing` for the folder they create (`--store` / `--checkpoint` are output flags). (4) `monday_check` no longer FAILs the session verdict outside market hours. (5) `tsfm_nightly` / `tsfm_probe` skip cleanly when the worker is not started. (6) G1 footer names the failing check; one G1 budget (15 ms, `G1_BUDGET_MS`). (7) slippage line states its sign. `docs/GROUP470_RUN_OUTPUT_FIXES.md`.
- group463: overall UI polish (frontend only): skip link, per-tab ErrorBoundary around the page area, toast live region, shared EmptyState, sticky/zebra tables, card depth, themed scrollbars. design-v4 tests 6 pass; syntax-only tsc clean. Doc: `docs/GROUP463_UI_POLISH.md`.
- group462: phase 4 finished (frontend only): price roll on the Surprise Stocks price column, lazy painting of Watchlist tiles. Spinners inside action buttons stay by design. Syntax-only tsc clean; design-v4 tests 5 pass. Doc: `docs/GROUP461_UI_PHASES_3_TO_5.md`.
- group461: login redesign phases 3-5 (frontend only).
